** 默认居中: org-mode 常驻 visual-fill-column
不用 writeroom 也能居中。=visual-fill-column-mode= 就是 writeroom 底层用来居中的那个包，
单独开它 **只做居中**：不放大字体、不切 mixed-pitch、不动 modeline、不独占窗口。

- 栏宽沿用 =writeroom-width= 的 100（中英混排，约 50 个汉字）
- 窗口本身比栏宽窄时它会自动放弃居中（正常行为，不是配置没生效）
- ⚠️ =visual-fill-column-width= / =-center-text= 都被 =(make-variable-buffer-local)=
  标记过，普通 ~setq~ **只改当前 buffer**（启动时是 =*scratch*=），org buffer 根本拿不到。
  必须 ~setq-default~。writeroom 之所以用 setq 也生效，是因为它在目标 buffer 里执行的。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package! visual-fill-column
  :hook (org-mode . visual-fill-column-mode)
  :config
  ;; 中英混排: 汉字宽度约为英文两倍, 80 列只剩 ~40 个汉字, 太窄
  (setq-default visual-fill-column-width 100
                visual-fill-column-center-text t))

(defun my/org-recenter-after-writeroom ()
  "Restore centered layout after `writeroom-mode' is turned off.
`writeroom--disable' calls (visual-fill-column-mode -1) and kills the
buffer-local width/center variables, so an org buffer would go back to
left-aligned after leaving zen.  This puts it back."
  (when (derived-mode-p 'org-mode)
    (setq-local visual-fill-column-width 100)
    (setq-local visual-fill-column-center-text t)
    (visual-fill-column-mode 1)))
#+end_src

** 宽表格显示: 手动切「阅读布局 ↔ 表格布局」

宽表格行（本库常见 180--234 列）在默认的居中软换行布局下会被折成 2--3 个
视觉行，管道符错位、整表读不成。=SPC t W= 在当前 buffer 两种布局间切换：

- *阅读布局*（默认）：=visual-fill-column-mode= 栏宽 100 居中 + 软换行，
  长散文舒适；宽表格折行。
- *表格布局*：关软换行 + =truncate-lines t= + 撤居中边距（满窗宽），
  表格保持单逻辑行；右侧溢出用 ~C-x >~ / ~C-x <~ 横向滚动（fringe 有箭头提示）。
  配合 ~C-x C---~ 缩小字号还能多塞约 1/4 列。

2026-10-17 做过「宽表格进入视野就自动切换」的版本，Sean 实测「表现太差」
撤掉了（滚动时布局反复横跳）。所以这里只做手动开关：零 hook、零自动切换，
只影响当前 buffer，新开的 org buffer 仍是默认阅读布局。

⚠️ 顺序坑（2026-10-17 实测，Emacs 31 =simple.el=:8726-8729）：
关 =visual-line-mode= 的分支会 ~kill-local-variable~ 掉 =truncate-lines= 并
恢复旧值 —— 必须**先关模式再设 truncate**，顺序反了等于白设。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(defun my/org-wide-table-layout-toggle ()
  "切换当前 org buffer：阅读布局（居中软换行）↔ 表格布局（整行截断）。
表格布局下宽表格保持单逻辑行，右侧溢出用 `C-x >' / `C-x <' 横滚。
只影响当前 buffer；新开的 org buffer 仍是默认阅读布局。
zen（writeroom）开启时先临时退出 zen，切回阅读布局时恢复。"
  (interactive)
  (unless (derived-mode-p 'org-mode)
    (user-error "仅 org-mode buffer 可用"))
  (if (bound-and-true-p my/org-wide-table-layout)
      ;; ---- 回到阅读布局 ----
      (progn
        (setq-local my/org-wide-table-layout nil)
        (if (bound-and-true-p my/org-wide-table--writeroom)
            (progn
              (setq-local my/org-wide-table--writeroom nil)
              (visual-line-mode 1)
              (writeroom-mode 1))
          (visual-line-mode 1)
          ;; 复用 zen 退出后的恢复逻辑：重设 100 栏宽 + 居中 + 开 vfc
          (my/org-recenter-after-writeroom)))
    ;; ---- 切到表格布局 ----
    (setq-local my/org-wide-table-layout t)
    (when (bound-and-true-p writeroom-mode)
      (setq-local my/org-wide-table--writeroom t)
      (writeroom-mode -1))
    ;; 必须先关 visual-line-mode：它的关停分支会 kill-local-variable
    ;; truncate-lines 并恢复旧值，顺序反了被静默冲掉（2026-10-17 实测）
    (visual-line-mode -1)
    (setq-local truncate-lines t)
    (visual-fill-column-mode -1))
  (message "org 布局: %s（%s）"
           (if (bound-and-true-p my/org-wide-table-layout) "表格/截断" "阅读/居中软换行")
           (if (bound-and-true-p my/org-wide-table-layout)
               "C-x > 横滚看右侧, C-x C-- 缩字号"
             "宽表格会折行, SPC t W 再切")))

(map! :leader
      :prefix "t"
      "W" #'my/org-wide-table-layout-toggle)
#+end_src

** Writeroom: 居中栏宽 (替代 olivetti)
=:ui zen= 模块自带的 writeroom-mode 已经装好了，不用再加包。
Doom 默认键位：=SPC t z= 开关 zen，=SPC t Z= zen + 全屏独占。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(after! writeroom-mode
  ;; 中英混排: 汉字宽度约为英文两倍, Doom 默认的 80 列只剩 ~40 个汉字, 太窄
  (setq writeroom-width 100
        ;; 只影响当前 buffer, 不留全局副作用(Doom 已把 global-effects 置 nil)
        writeroom-maximize-window nil
        writeroom-mode-line t)
  ;; 正文居中而不是贴左边缘; 这是 buffer-local 变量, 必须 setq-default
  (setq-default visual-fill-column-center-text t)
  ;; 中文写作放大一档; 改 0 就是原尺寸
  (setq +zen-text-scale 1.0)
  ;; 退出 zen 后回到普通居中(而不是贴左), 见 [默认居中]
  (add-hook 'writeroom-mode-disable-hook #'my/org-recenter-after-writeroom))

;; 自动进去太打扰, 但要有个顺手的入口: 见 [[#my-focus-toggle][Focus mode toggle]]
#+end_src

** Figure: 图片模板与图注编号
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(defvar my/org-caption-number-format "图 %d: "
  "Display string prepended to each #+CAPTION by `my/org-caption-number-mode'.")

(defvar my/org-caption-number--timer nil
  "Idle timer used to throttle caption renumbering.")

(defun my/org-caption-number--clear (&optional buf)
  "Remove caption-number overlays from BUF (default: current buffer)."
  (with-current-buffer (or buf (current-buffer))
    (dolist (ov (overlays-in (point-min) (point-max)))
      (when (eq (overlay-get ov 'category) 'my/org-caption-number)
        (delete-overlay ov)))))

(defun my/org-caption-number-refresh (&optional buf)
  "Number every #+CAPTION in BUF with a temporary overlay.
File contents are NOT modified; numbering is display-only, so it never
collides with what Org itself generates on export."
  (interactive)
  (with-current-buffer (or buf (current-buffer))
    (unless (derived-mode-p 'org-mode)
      (user-error "Not in an Org buffer"))
    (my/org-caption-number--clear (current-buffer))
    (let ((n 0))
      (save-excursion
        (goto-char (point-min))
        (while (re-search-forward "^\\(#\\+CAPTION:\\)" nil t)
          ;; 跳过代码块里用于示范的 org 片段
          (unless (org-between-regexps-p "^#\\+begin_src" "^#\\+end_src")
            (setq n (1+ n))
            (let ((ov (make-overlay (match-beginning 1) (match-end 1))))
              (overlay-put ov 'category 'my/org-caption-number)
              (overlay-put ov 'display (format my/org-caption-number-format n))
              (overlay-put ov 'face 'bold)
              (overlay-put ov 'evaporate t)
              (overlay-put ov 'modification-hooks
                           (list (lambda (ov _after _b _e &optional _len)
                                   ;; 用户真的动这一行时撤掉伪装, 露出原始文本
                                   (delete-overlay ov))))))))
      n)))

(defun my/org-caption-number--schedule (&rest _)
  "Renumber captions shortly after editing stops (avoids per-keystroke cost)."
  (when (and (bound-and-true-p my/org-caption-number-mode)
             (derived-mode-p 'org-mode))
    (when (timerp my/org-caption-number--timer)
      (cancel-timer my/org-caption-number--timer))
    (setq my/org-caption-number--timer
          (run-at-time 0.4 nil
                       (lambda (b) (when (buffer-live-p b)
                                     (my/org-caption-number-refresh b)))
                       (current-buffer)))))

;;;###autoload
(define-minor-mode my/org-caption-number-mode
  "Show 图 N: in front of every #+CAPTION via temporary overlays.
The buffer text is untouched, so Org's own export numbering still works."
  :init-value nil
  :lighter " 图N"
  (if my/org-caption-number-mode
      (progn (my/org-caption-number-refresh)
             (add-hook 'after-change-functions #'my/org-caption-number--schedule nil t))
    (my/org-caption-number--clear)
    (remove-hook 'after-change-functions #'my/org-caption-number--schedule t)))

(defun my/org-insert-image-figure (file &optional width)
  "Insert FILE as a captioned figure and leave point on the caption line.
FILE is stored relative to the current buffer's directory.
If point is already on an image link, caption that one instead.
With a numeric prefix WIDTH, also emit an #+ATTR_ORG :width line."
  (interactive
   (list (unless (my/org-image-file-at-point)
           (read-file-name "Image file: " nil nil t nil
                           (lambda (f)
                             (or (file-directory-p f)
                                 (string-match-p
                                  "\\.\\(png\\|jpe?g\\|gif\\|webp\\|svg\\|bmp\\|tiff?\\)$"
                                  (downcase f))))))
         (and current-prefix-arg (prefix-numeric-value current-prefix-arg))))
  (let ((existing (my/org-image-file-at-point)))
    (if existing
        ;; 就地给已有图片加 figure 骨架
        (progn
          (my/org-image-caption)
          (message "Caption inserted for %s" (file-name-nondirectory existing)))
      ;; 插入新图: caption + 可选宽度 + 链接
      (unless (and file (file-exists-p file))
        (user-error "No such image file"))
      (let ((rel (file-relative-name (expand-file-name file)
                                     (file-name-directory buffer-file-name))))
        (insert "#+CAPTION: \n")
        (when (and width (numberp width) (> width 0))
          (insert (format "#+ATTR_ORG: :width %d\n" width)))
        (insert (format "[[file:%s]]\n" rel))
        (forward-line (if width -3 -2))
        (end-of-line)
        (my/org-display-images)
        (message "Figure inserted: %s" rel)))))
#+end_src

** org-attach
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(after! org
  (require 'org-attach)

  ;; 目录 = attach 年月 (2026-10-20 起, 取代平铺池)。
  ;; 内置那两个 ID->路径函数**都不校验 ID 格式**, 只判长度:
  ;;   org-attach-id-uuid-folder-format: (< 2 (length id)) -> <前2>/<余下>
  ;;   org-attach-id-ts-folder-format:   (< 6 (length id)) -> <前6>/<余下>
  ;; 于是 list 顺序是个互斥的二元选择 —— uuid 在前会把 ts ID 切成
  ;; 20/260430T…, ts 在前会把 UUID 切成 5f2162/9a-…。本库 ID 是混合的
  ;; (文件级 ts + heading 级 UUID), 两边都会错, 所以自己写一个显式判格式。
  ;; 注: ID 取该 heading 最早附件的时间戳, 目录名因此是真实的 attach 年月,
  ;; 不是「建 ID 那天」—— 2026-10-20 迁移时 55 个附件是这么定的。
  ;; 2026-10-24 补: UUID 分支只是兼容存量。read/media 库 927 个 uuid5
  ;; heading 一被拖附件就掉进 <前2>/<余下>, 掉出年月布局 (2026-10-24
  ;; 三千美娇娘 掉进 data/0a/… 就这么来的)。现在 attach 前
  ;; `my/org-attach--get-create-a' (下面统一收口一节) 会把非 ts 形态的
  ;; 有效 ID 归一成 ts, 新附件只走 ts 分支; 这个分支留给历史遗留的
  ;; UUID 目录解析。
  (defun my/org-attach-id-folder-format (id)
    "`<YYYYMM>/<rest>' for timestamp IDs, `<2>/<rest>' for UUIDs."
    (cond ((string-match-p "\\`[0-9]\\{8\\}T[0-9]\\{6\\}" id)
           (format "%s/%s" (substring id 0 6) (substring id 6)))
          ((string-match-p "\\`[0-9a-f]\\{8\\}-[0-9a-f]\\{4\\}-" id)
           (format "%s/%s" (substring id 0 2) (substring id 2)))
          (t (org-attach-id-fallback-folder-format id))))

  (setq org-attach-id-dir (expand-file-name "data/" org-directory)
        org-attach-id-to-path-function-list '(my/org-attach-id-folder-format)
        org-attach-method 'cp
        org-attach-use-inheritance t
        org-attach-store-link-p 'attached
        org-attach-auto-tag nil
        ;; Emacs 30/Org 9.7+ 才有: 拖放与粘贴的图一律进 org-attach,
        ;; 由下面 ** org-attach 统一收口 里的 advice 统一命名。
        org-yank-dnd-method 'attach
        org-yank-image-save-method 'attach)

  ;; Fix attachment 链接中文乱码
  (defun my/org-attach-store-link-decoded (&rest _)
    "Fix stored links from `org-attach-attach' to include decoded description."
    (when org-stored-links
      (let ((latest (car org-stored-links)))
        (when (and (stringp (car latest))
                   (string-prefix-p "attachment:" (car latest))
                   (or (null (cadr latest)) (string= (cadr latest) "")))
          (setcar (cdr latest)
                  (decode-coding-string
                   (url-unhex-string
                    (file-name-nondirectory
                     (substring (car latest) (length "attachment:"))))
                   'utf-8))))))
  (advice-add 'org-attach-attach :after #'my/org-attach-store-link-decoded))
#+end_src

** 文件名生成（denote 风格，但不依赖 denote 包）
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
;; 2026-10-17: denote 包已卸载，但 ~/org/data/ 里已有一千多个
;; `<id>--<slug>__<kw1>_<kw2>.ext' 命名的附件，格式必须延续（排序、去重、
;; 人工可读性都靠它）。这里自己实现一份，行为对齐 denote:
;;   id   = %Y%m%dT%H%M%S 时间戳
;;   slug = 非字母数字转连字符（保留 CJK，跟标题里的中文一致），首尾连字符去掉，
;;          且一律 downcase —— 实测 denote 对标题段也 downcase，历史文件全是小写
;;   kw   = 每个关键词 sluggify + 小写，用 _ 连接，整体前缀 __
;;   keywords 为空 → 省略 __ 段
(defconst my/date-identifier-format "%Y%m%dT%H%M%S"
  "时间戳 ID 格式，沿用 denote identifier，保证与历史附件名可一起排序。")

(defconst my/known-keywords
  '(;; 工作 / 生活分界
    "work"
    ;; 领域
    "gamedev" "emacs" "ai" "pkm" "investment" "health" "habit" "read" "hire" "media"
    ;; 2026-10-24 补（Sean 拍板）：当时 notes/ 下已有 linux/ 与 device/ 两个域目录
    ;; （各 5 文件），白名单缺它们 → 这 10 个文件 denote 改名时拿不到 keyword。
    ;; misc 不加：misc 语义就是「无 keyword」，为孤例开容器违反 Voit 原则。
    ;; 2026-10-25：linux/ 域目录已与 software/ emacs/ pkm/ 合并成 software/,
    ;; 但 "linux" keyword **必须留** —— 那 5 篇笔记文件名后缀 __linux 还在,
    ;; 且 data/ 里可能有同名 keyword 的附件。keyword 与目录 2026-10-25 起解耦。
    "linux" "device"
    ;; 状态 / 跨域标记
    "idea" "memo" "tool" "project" "index"
    ;; 形态
    "journal" "document" "movie" "tv" "anime" "music" "game" "book" "manga")
  "关键词白名单，只用于附件命名时的补全（原 denote-known-keywords 的镜像）。
笔记本身已经不再用 keyword 分类——那是大文件体系之前的事。")

;; 两个 sluggify 各自复刻 denote 的对应函数（denote.el 1101 / 1187 / 1199）。
;; 关键点: 只有空格和下划线转连字符，**其余非字母数字字符原样保留**，
;; 所以《》（）这些全角标点会留在文件名里 —— data/ 里那 3 个历史附件
;; （《艳遇修真》（珍藏最终版1-263章）等）就是这个规则，别改成「非字母数字
;; 一律转连字符」，否则新名字跟老名字对不上。
(defun my/sluggify-title (title)
  "Slug of TITLE for the file-name title part.  Replaces `denote-sluggify-title'."
  (let ((s (replace-regexp-in-string "[][{}!@#$%^&*()+'\"?,.\|;:~`‘’“”/=]*" "" title)))
    (setq s (replace-regexp-in-string "_\\|\\s-+" "-" s))
    (setq s (replace-regexp-in-string "-\\{2,\\}" "-" s))
    (downcase (replace-regexp-in-string "^-\\|-$" "" s))))

(defun my/sluggify-keyword (keyword)
  "Slug of KEYWORD: lowercased, punctuation dropped.  Replaces `denote-sluggify-keyword'."
  (downcase (replace-regexp-in-string "[][{}!@#$%^&*()+'\"?,.\|;:~`‘’“”/_ =-]*" "" keyword)))

(defun my/keywords-prompt (&optional prompt)
  "Read keywords with completion.  Replacement for `denote-keywords-prompt'."
  (let ((input (completing-read-multiple
                (or prompt "Keywords (逗号分隔, TAB 补全, 直接回车=无): ")
                my/known-keywords nil nil)))
    (mapcar #'my/sluggify-keyword input)))

(defun my/org-file-name-format (filename &optional keywords)
  "Rewrite FILENAME to denote style: <id>--<slug>__<keywords><ext>.
KEYWORDS 非空才带 __ 段。不依赖 denote 包，格式与它保持一致。"
  (let* ((ext   (or (file-name-extension filename t) ""))
         (base  (or (file-name-sans-extension filename) ""))
         (id    (format-time-string my/date-identifier-format))
         (slug  (my/sluggify-title base))
         (kwstr (if keywords
                    (concat "__" (mapconcat #'my/sluggify-keyword keywords "_"))
                  "")))
    (concat id "--" slug kwstr ext)))
#+end_src

** org-attach 统一收口（所有附件只走 org-attach-attach）
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
;; 思路来自 https://gist.github.com/yibie/9bd280522c24eec41ce0b32b327967cd
;; 粘贴 / 截图 / 拖放 / org-download / 手动 attach 全部汇到 org-attach-attach,
;; 命名规则与「复制还是移动」只写一遍。针对本机改了三处:
;;   1. macOS 依赖 (pngpaste/qlmanage/Finder) -> wl-paste/xclip + xdg-open
;;   2. 命名换成 denote 风格 (复用 my/org-file-name-format)
;;   3. 不做无条件 mv —— gist 那样会把从文件管理器拖进来的原件删掉
(after! org
  (require 'org-attach)

  ;;; 1. 公共函数: 解析 file:/attachment: 链接的真实路径
  ;; 别拿 org-attach-id-dir 当基准目录 —— entry 可以用 DIR 属性覆盖它,
  ;; 只有 (org-attach-dir) 才是该 heading 真正的目录。
  ;; (之前三处硬编码 org-attach-id-dir, 而真实目录是 data/<YYYYMM>/<余下>/,
  ;; 于是 attachment: 链接一律被判成「文件不存在」—— 删链接的命令还会照样把链接吃掉。)
  (defun my/org-link-file-at-point ()
    "Return the absolute path of the file/attachment link at point, or nil.
`file:' 相对 `default-directory', `attachment:' 相对该 heading 真正的
attach 目录。"
    (let* ((ctx  (org-element-context))
           (type (org-element-property :type ctx))
           (path (when (member type '("file" "attachment"))
                   (org-element-property :path ctx))))
      (when path
        (expand-file-name (org-link-unescape path)
                          (if (equal type "attachment")
                              (my/org-attach--dir)
                            default-directory)))))

  (defun my/org-attach--dir ()
    "The attachment directory in use, falling back to the pool root.
2026-10-20 起附件按 entry 分目录 (data/<YYYYMM>/<ID余下>/), `org-attach-dir'
对「既无 ID 也无 DIR」的条目仍然返回 nil —— 那时回落 org-attach-id-dir:
data/ 顶层还躺着 82 个无主孤儿, 老链接有可能指向那里。
末尾斜杠统一去掉, 跟 `org-attach-dir' 自己的风格一致。"
    (directory-file-name
     (or (org-attach-dir)
         org-attach-id-dir)))

  ;; 2026-10-20: 上面这个兜底原先做在 org-attach-dir 自己的 advice 里
  ;; (平铺时代: 所有条目共用 data/, 于是「没有 ID」不等于「没有目录」)。
  ;; 分目录之后那个 advice 必须撤掉, 否则 org-attach-dir 对无 ID 条目永远返回
  ;; 池子, org-attach-dir-get-create 就不会给它造 ID —— 新附件会继续落进平铺
  ;; 池, 目录结构立刻腐化。撤掉之后 org 自己的解析
  ;; (org-attach-expand -> (expand-file-name file (org-attach-dir))) 对无 ID 条目
  ;; 拿到 nil, expand-file-name 会把它当相对 default-directory 解析 ——
  ;; 2026-10-18 实测过: 路径变成 ~/org/<文件名> 而不是 ~/org/data/<文件名>,
  ;; ol.el 的 org-link-preview-file 里 (file-exists-p file) 为 nil, 函数
  ;; **静默返回 nil**: 不显示、不报错、echo area 干干净净。全库 47 张图里
  ;; 37 张是这么丢的。分目录之后「有附件却没有 ID」的条目已经不存在了
  ;; (迁移时给 31 个挂载 heading 全部注入了 ID), 剩下的无 ID 条目本来就没链接。

  ;;; 2. 命名: 可读名保留, 机器名转 denote 名
  (defvar my/org-attach-generated-name-regexps
    '("\\`[0-9a-f]\\{16,\\}\\'"                            ; hash
      "\\`[0-9a-f]\\{8\\}-[0-9a-f]\\{4\\}-[0-9a-f-]+\\'"    ; uuid
      "\\`[0-9_. -]+\\'"                                    ; 纯数字/日期
      "\\`wechatimg[0-9]+\\'"                               ; 微信导出 (英文名)
      "\\`微信图片[0-9_]*\\'"                                ; 微信导出 (中文名)
      "\\`\\(mmexport\\|wx_camera_[0-9]+\\)"                ; 微信导出
      "\\`[0-9]+_\\.pic\\(_hd\\)?\\'"                       ; 微博图床
      "\\`\\(img\\|dsc\\|pxl\\)[_-]?[0-9]+"                 ; 相机
      "\\`clipboard-"
      "\\`\\(screenshot\\|screen shot\\|截屏\\|截图\\|屏幕截图\\|屏幕快照\\)"
      "\\`\\(image\\|img\\|download\\|unnamed\\|untitled\\|file\\)\\([ _-]?[0-9]*\\)\\'"
      "\\`\\(screencapture\\|flameshot\\|snipaste\\)[ _-]?[0-9]*") ; Linux 截图工具
    "Base names (no extension, case-insensitive) that say nothing about the content.")

  (defvar my/org-attach-slug-length 24
    "Maximum width of the heading part of a generated file name.")

  (defun my/org-attach--slug ()
    "The current heading (or the file title) squeezed into a file-name fragment."
    (let* ((title (or (and (derived-mode-p 'org-mode)
                           (not (org-before-first-heading-p))
                           (org-get-heading t t t t))
                      (and (derived-mode-p 'org-mode)
                           (cadar (org-collect-keywords '("TITLE"))))
                      ""))
           (text (if (string-match org-link-bracket-re title)
                     (replace-regexp-in-string org-link-bracket-re
                                               (lambda (m) (or (match-string 2 m) ""))
                                               title)
                   title))
           (slug (string-trim (replace-regexp-in-string "[^[:alnum:]]+" "-" text)
                              "-+" "-+")))
      (string-trim-right
       (truncate-string-to-width slug my/org-attach-slug-length) "-+")))

  (defvar my/org-attach-slug-min-width 3
    "Minimum `string-width' for a generated slug to count as information.")

  (defun my/org-attach--slug-useful-p (slug)
    "Non-nil when SLUG says something about the file.
大文件体系下一个 heading 常常就叫 `12.11'、`天气'、`一张图', 直接拿它做 slug
会产出 `20260506T013013--12-11.jpg' 这种比原文件名还糟的东西 —— 这时宁可原样
保留 `20260913_104934.jpg'。"
    (and slug
         (>= (string-width slug) my/org-attach-slug-min-width)
         ;; 纯数字 / 纯日期: 12-11, 2026-10-17
         (not (string-match-p "\\`[0-9]+\\(-[0-9]+\\)*\\'" slug))))

  (defun my/org-attach--generated-name-p (base)
    "Non-nil when BASE (no extension) looks machine-generated."
    (let ((case-fold-search t))
      (seq-some (lambda (re) (string-match-p re base))
                my/org-attach-generated-name-regexps)))

  (defun my/org-attach-name-for-file (file)
    "The name FILE should get: kept if readable, else <id>--<slug>.ext.
机器名转成 20261013T235000--标题.ext, 复用 `my/org-file-name-format'。
这也是 `org-download-file-format-function'。

2026-10-17 起**不再带 __keyword 段**。以前那段取的是 heading 当时的 tag
(`my/org-attach--keywords'), 而 tag 会改、会删、条目会 refile, 文件名却不跟着
更新 —— 半年后 `__health' 就是一条错误线索。归因靠笔记正文和 CUSTOM_ID,
共享池不负责分类。附带效果: 改名频率下降, `my/org-attach-rename' 要重写的链接
也变少。"
    (let* ((name (file-name-nondirectory file))
           (base (file-name-sans-extension name)))
      (if (not (my/org-attach--generated-name-p base))
          name
        (let ((slug (my/org-attach--slug)))
          (my/org-file-name-format
           (concat (if (my/org-attach--slug-useful-p slug) slug base)
                   (file-name-extension name t)))))))

  (defun my/org-attach--unique-name (name dir)
    "Return NAME, or NAME with -2, -3… before the extension if DIR has it already."
    (let ((base (file-name-sans-extension name))
          (ext  (file-name-extension name t))
          (n 1)
          (candidate name))
      (while (file-exists-p (expand-file-name candidate dir))
        (setq n (1+ n)
              candidate (format "%s-%d%s" base n ext)))
      candidate))

  ;;; 3. 收口: :around advice
  (defvar my/org-attach-move-from-dirs
    (list temporary-file-directory)
    "Only files inside these directories are MOVED, not copied.
gist 是无条件 mv —— 那在拖放场景是灾难: 从文件管理器拖个 PDF 进来原件就没了。
这里默认只移临时目录 (粘贴/截图/URL 下载都落在那里)。想让 Downloads 也移走:
  (add-to-list 'my/org-attach-move-from-dirs (expand-file-name \"~/Downloads\"))")

  (defun my/org-attach--movable-p (file)
    "Non-nil when FILE may be moved instead of copied."
    (let ((dir (expand-file-name (file-name-directory (expand-file-name file)))))
      (seq-some (lambda (d)
                  (and d (string-prefix-p (expand-file-name d) dir)))
                my/org-attach-move-from-dirs)))

  (defun my/org-attach--attach-a (orig file &optional visit-dir method)
    "Around advice for `org-attach-attach': uniform names, mv only from staging.
Only regular local files attached with cp/mv are touched; ln, lns, url, none
and directories are passed to ORIG unchanged."
    (let ((effective (or method org-attach-method)))
      (if (or (not (memq effective '(cp mv)))
              (not (file-regular-p file)))
          (funcall orig file visit-dir method)
        (let* ((file (expand-file-name file))
               (name (my/org-attach--unique-name
                      (my/org-attach-name-for-file file)
                      (org-attach-dir 'get-create))))
          (if (string= name (file-name-nondirectory file))
              ;; 名字不用改: 只决定 mv 还是 cp
              (funcall orig file visit-dir
                       (if (my/org-attach--movable-p file) 'mv 'cp))
            ;; 改名: 先复制到暂存目录再 attach, 成功之后才动原件,
            ;; 中途失败什么都不会丢。
            (let* ((stage  (make-temp-file "org-attach" t))
                   (staged (expand-file-name name stage)))
              (unwind-protect
                  (progn
                    (copy-file file staged nil t)
                    (prog1 (funcall orig staged visit-dir 'mv)
                      (when (my/org-attach--movable-p file)
                        (delete-file file))))
                (delete-directory stage t))))))))

  (advice-add 'org-attach-attach :around #'my/org-attach--attach-a)

  ;;; 3b. 目录定生死: attach 前把非 ts 形态的 ID 归一成 ts (2026-10-24)
  ;; 两条拖放入口 —— org--dnd-local-file-handler → org-attach-attach 与
  ;; XDS 的 org--dnd-xds-function → (org-attach-dir 'create) —— 唯一的
  ;; 交汇点就是 org-attach-dir-get-create, advice 挂在这里两条都盖住
  ;; (XDS 那条根本不过 org-attach-attach, 只堵在 org-attach-attach 上
  ;;  等于没堵; 书单里那条裸 [[attachment:名]] 链接就是 XDS 的产物)。
  ;; 病症: read/media 库 927 个 heading 带 10-18 uuid5 批量的 UUID ID,
  ;; 而 UUID 的附件路径是 <前2>/<余下> —— 一拖附件就掉出「目录 = attach
  ;; 年月」。口径照 10-20 迁移: 「ID 取该 heading 最早附件的时间戳」,
  ;; 这里落成常态。新 ID 走 org-id-new (org-id-method 是 'ts, 唯一性
  ;; 由 my/org-id-new-unique 顺延保证, 不用自己查表)。
  ;; 不碰的: ① 已是 ts 形态的有效 ID (含继承 —— 继承 ts 的条目附件照旧
  ;; 进父条目目录); ② DIR/ATTACH_DIR 显式指定目录的; ③ 无 ID 条目
  ;; (org-attach-dir-get-create 自己会经 org-id-get 造 ts ID);
  ;; ④ bobp 文件级抽屉 (只在 heading 上动, 同 org-roam-extract 补丁口径)。
  (defun my/org-attach--ts-id-p (id)
    "Non-nil when ID is a `my/date-identifier-format' timestamp."
    (and (stringp id)
         (string-match-p "\\`[0-9]\\{8\\}T[0-9]\\{6\\}\\'" id)))

  (defun my/org-attach--normalize-id ()
    "Rewrite the current entry's ID to timestamp form before attaching.
UUID IDs map to <2>/<rest> attachment dirs, breaking the year-month
layout; see the comment above for the full rationale.  Entries with no
ID at all are deliberately left alone: `org-attach-dir-get-create' then
goes through `org-id-get', which creates *and registers* a timestamp ID
anyway (`org-id-method' is 'ts)."
    (unless (or (org-before-first-heading-p)
                (org-entry-get nil "DIR" org-attach-use-inheritance)
                (org-entry-get nil "ATTACH_DIR" org-attach-use-inheritance))
      (let ((eff (org-entry-get nil "ID" org-attach-use-inheritance)))
        (when (and eff (not (my/org-attach--ts-id-p eff)))
          (org-entry-put nil "ID"
                         (let ((id (org-id-new)))
                           (if (my/org-attach--ts-id-p id)
                               id
                             (format-time-string my/date-identifier-format))))))))

  (defun my/org-attach--get-create-a (orig)
    ":around advice for `org-attach-dir-get-create': normalize ID first.
Both drag-and-drop entry points funnel through this function, so this
is the single place that guarantees every new attachment directory is
keyed by a timestamp ID, hence lives under data/<YYYYMM>/."
    (my/org-attach--normalize-id)
    (funcall orig))

  (advice-add 'org-attach-dir-get-create :around #'my/org-attach--get-create-a)

  ;;; 4. Linux 剪贴板取图 (Wayland 优先, X11 兜底)
  (defun my/org-attach--clipboard-image-to (file)
    "Write the clipboard image to FILE.  Return non-nil on success."
    (let ((cmd (cond ((executable-find "wl-paste")
                      (format "wl-paste --no-newline --type image/png > %s"
                              (shell-quote-argument file)))
                     ((executable-find "xclip")
                      (format "xclip -selection clipboard -t image/png -o > %s"
                              (shell-quote-argument file))))))
      (and cmd
           (zerop (call-process-shell-command cmd nil nil))
           (file-regular-p file)
           (> (file-attribute-size (file-attributes file)) 0))))

  (defun my/org-attach--insert-link (name &optional width)
    "Insert a link to attachment NAME.  Images get no description, so they inline.
预览走 `my/org-display-images', 不用 org-link-preview-region —— 本机统一封装过,
重复调用会叠加 overlay。
WIDTH 为正数时先在链接前插一行 #+ATTR_ORG: :width (格式同
`my/org-insert-image-figure'); 窗口 resize 时手动宽度会被保留, 见
`my/org-image-refresh-displayed'。"
    (let ((image (string-match-p (image-file-name-regexp) name)))
      (when (and width (numberp width) (> width 0))
        (insert (format "#+ATTR_ORG: :width %d\n" width)))
      (insert (org-link-make-string (concat "attachment:" name)
                                    (unless image name)))
      (when image (my/org-display-images))))

  (defvar my/org-paste-image-width 300
    "`my/org-attach-paste-image' 默认写的 #+ATTR_ORG :width (像素)。
设成 nil 或 0 = 不写宽度行, 图片显示交给 org-image-max-width (0.85 窗宽) 统一管。")

  (defun my/org-attach-paste-image (&optional width)
    "Attach the clipboard image to the current heading and insert a link.
WIDTH 为正数时链接前插一行 #+ATTR_ORG: :width; 不给就用 `my/org-paste-image-width'。
交互: 无前缀 = 默认宽度, 数字前缀 = 直接用它, C-u = 询问。"
    (interactive
     (list (cond ((consp current-prefix-arg)
                  (read-number "Width: " my/org-paste-image-width))
                 (current-prefix-arg
                  (prefix-numeric-value current-prefix-arg))
                 (t my/org-paste-image-width))))
    (let ((tmp (expand-file-name
                (format-time-string "clipboard-%Y%m%dT%H%M%S.png")
                temporary-file-directory)))
      (unless (my/org-attach--clipboard-image-to tmp)
        (user-error "No image on the clipboard"))
      (my/org-attach--insert-link
       (string-remove-prefix "attachment:"
                             (car (org-attach-attach tmp nil 'mv)))
       width)))

  ;;; 4b. normal/visual 态 `p' 自动分流: 剪贴板是图就贴图, 否则原样 evil 粘贴
  ;; 判据只认 image/png —— 跟 `my/org-attach--clipboard-image-to' 的取图能力
  ;; 严格对齐: 「检测说有图、提取却失败」(只有 jpeg 的剪贴板) 比漏检更烦人。
  ;; wl-paste 只问类型不取数据; 文本剪贴板时开销 = 一次 spawn。
  (defun my/clipboard-image-p ()
    "Non-nil when the clipboard offers an image/png selection."
    (catch 'found
      (let ((exe (executable-find "wl-paste")))
        (when exe
          (with-temp-buffer
            (when (zerop (ignore-errors
                           (call-process exe nil t nil "--list-types")))
              (goto-char (point-min))
              (while (re-search-forward "^image/png$" nil t)
                (throw 'found t))))))
      (let ((exe (executable-find "xclip")))
        (when exe
          (with-temp-buffer
            (when (zerop (ignore-errors
                           (call-process exe nil t nil "-selection" "clipboard"
                                         "-t" "TARGETS" "-o")))
              (goto-char (point-min))
              (while (re-search-forward "^image/png$" nil t)
                (throw 'found t))))))
      ;; GUI 后端兜底 (X11 上没装 wl-paste/xclip 时); 终端里会报错, ignore 掉
      (when (ignore-errors (gui-backend-get-selection 'CLIPBOARD 'image/png))
        (throw 'found t))
      nil))

  (defun my/org-paste-image--width (&optional arg)
    "`p' 要用的宽度: C-u 询问, 否则 `my/org-paste-image-width'。"
    (if (consp arg)
        (read-number "Width: " my/org-paste-image-width)
      my/org-paste-image-width))

  (defun my/org-paste-after (&optional arg)
    "org-mode normal 态的 `p': 剪贴板有图 → attach + 带宽度插链接, 否则 `evil-paste-after'。
count/register/prefix 原样转发, 文本粘贴行为与裸 evil 完全一致。"
    (interactive "P")
    (if (my/clipboard-image-p)
        (my/org-attach-paste-image (my/org-paste-image--width arg))
      (let ((current-prefix-arg arg))
        (call-interactively 'evil-paste-after))))

  (defun my/org-visual-paste (&optional arg)
    "org-mode visual 态的 `p': 有图 → 替换选区为图片链接, 否则 `evil-visual-paste'。"
    (interactive "P")
    (if (my/clipboard-image-p)
        (progn
          (when (use-region-p)
            (delete-region (region-beginning) (region-end)))
          (my/org-attach-paste-image (my/org-paste-image--width arg)))
      (let ((current-prefix-arg arg))
        (call-interactively 'evil-visual-paste))))

  ;; evil 状态图优先级高于 org-mode-map, 必须经 evil 辅助键图下发
  ;; (map! 底层就是 general → evil-define-key 那套); 放在 after! org 里,
  ;; org-mode-map 此时已存在。已在跑的会话要重启 Emacs 才生效
  ;; (evil-normalize-keymaps 只在 evil(-local)-mode 启用时跑, reload 不补)。
  (map! :map org-mode-map
        :n "p" #'my/org-paste-after
        :v "p" #'my/org-visual-paste)

  ;;; 5. 命令: 插入已有链接 / 改名 / 最新下载 / 外部打开
  (defun my/org-attach--shared-dir-p (&optional dir)
    "Non-nil when DIR is the shared flat pool, not a per-entry directory.
2026-10-20 起绝大多数目录都是 per-entry 的, 这一关基本不再触发 —— 只有回落
到 org-attach-id-dir 的无 ID 条目(其附件躺在 82 个孤儿里)才会命中, 而那种
情况下「删整个目录」确实等于「删全部附件」, 守卫理应生效。"
    (let ((dir (or dir (my/org-attach--dir))))
      (string= (file-name-as-directory (expand-file-name dir))
                  (file-name-as-directory
                   (expand-file-name org-attach-id-dir)))))

  (defun my/org-attach--subtree-attachments ()
    "File names referenced by `attachment:' links in the current subtree.
分目录之后 `org-attach-file-list' 能直接列目录了, 但共享附件(同一个文件被
两个 heading 引用, 如染色体报告)仍然只有链接说得清, 所以保留这条路径。
链接删了文件就成了孤儿, 那是另一回事。"
    (let (names)
      (save-excursion
        (save-restriction
          (unless (org-before-first-heading-p) (org-narrow-to-subtree))
          (goto-char (point-min))
          ;; 非贪婪: [[attachment:a.jpg]] 与 [[attachment:a.jpg][说明]] 都能吃掉
          (while (re-search-forward
                  "\\[\\[attachment:\\([^]]+?\\)\\]\\(?:\\[[^]]*\\]\\)?\\]" nil t)
            (let ((name (org-link-unescape
                         (match-string-no-properties 1))))
              ;; 去掉 ::page 之类的后缀
              (when (string-match "::" name)
                (setq name (substring name 0 (match-beginning 0))))
              (unless (member name names)
                (push name names))))))
      (nreverse names)))

  (defun my/org-attach--files (&optional all)
    "Attachment file names of the current heading; error if there are none.
默认只认当前 subtree 里 attachment: 链接引用过的文件 —— 共享附件(同一个
文件被两个 heading 引用)只有链接说得清。ALL 非 nil (C-u) 时改列整个目录,
即 org-attach 眼里的归属, 想引用本目录里没被链接引用过的文件时用这个。"
    (if all
        (let ((dir (my/org-attach--dir)))
          (or (and dir (org-attach-file-list dir))
              (user-error "No attachment directory")))
      (let ((dir (my/org-attach--dir)))
        (or (seq-filter (lambda (n) (file-exists-p (expand-file-name n dir)))
                        (my/org-attach--subtree-attachments))
            (user-error "No attachments on this heading")))))

  (defun my/org-attach--read (prompt &optional all)
    "Pick one attachment of the current heading with PROMPT.
With a single attachment, return it without asking.  ALL 传给
`my/org-attach--files'."
    (let ((files (my/org-attach--files all)))
      (if (cdr files) (completing-read prompt files nil t) (car files))))

  (defun my/org-attach-open (&optional in-emacs)
    "Like `org-attach-open', but only offers this heading's attachments.
原版列整个目录, 平铺之后会冒出上百个无关文件。"
    (interactive "P")
    (let ((path (expand-file-name (my/org-attach--read "Open attachment: ")
                                  (my/org-attach--dir))))
      (run-hook-with-args 'org-attach-open-hook path)
      (org-open-file path in-emacs)))

  (defun my/org-attach-delete-one ()
    "Like `org-attach-delete-one', but only offers this heading's attachments."
    (interactive)
    (let* ((dir      (my/org-attach--dir))
           (name     (my/org-attach--read "Delete attachment: "))
           (file     (expand-file-name name dir)))
      (unless (and (file-exists-p file)
                   (y-or-n-p (format "Delete %s from %s? " name dir)))
        (user-error "Aborted"))
      (delete-file file)
      (run-hook-with-args 'org-attach-after-change-hook dir)
      (message "Deleted %s" name)))

  ;; org-attach-delete-all 会删掉整个 attach 目录 —— 平铺之后那就是全部附件。
  ;; 直接拦掉, 真要批量删请用 Dired。
  (defun my/org-attach-delete-all-guard (orig &rest args)
    "Refuse `org-attach-delete-all' when attachments live in the shared pool."
    (if (my/org-attach--shared-dir-p)
        (user-error "Attachments live in the shared pool %s; delete from Dired"
                    org-attach-id-dir)
      (apply orig args)))
  (advice-add 'org-attach-delete-all :around #'my/org-attach-delete-all-guard)

  (defun my/org-attach-insert-link ()
    "Insert a link to one of the current heading's attachments.
C-u: 从整个 ~/org/data/ 池子里挑 (引用别处已有的文件)。"
    (interactive)
    (my/org-attach--insert-link
     (my/org-attach--read "Insert link to: " current-prefix-arg)))

  (defun my/org-attach-rename ()
    "Rename an attachment and rewrite the links to it in this subtree."
    (interactive)
    (let* ((dir  (my/org-attach--dir))
           (old  (my/org-attach--read "Rename attachment: " current-prefix-arg))
           (ext  (file-name-extension old t))
           (base (read-string (format "New name (%s is kept): " ext)
                              (file-name-sans-extension old)))
           (new  (concat (file-name-sans-extension
                          (if (string-suffix-p ext base) base (concat base ext)))
                         ext)))
      (when (string-empty-p (file-name-sans-extension new))
        (user-error "The name cannot be empty"))
      (unless (string= old new)
        ;; 池子是共用的, 撞名会静默覆盖别人的文件 —— 宁可报错。
        (when (file-exists-p (expand-file-name new dir))
          (user-error "%s already exists in %s" new dir))
        (rename-file (expand-file-name old dir) (expand-file-name new dir))
        (let ((count 0)
              (re (concat "\\(attachment:\\)"
                          (regexp-opt (list old (org-link-escape old)))
                          "\\(\\]\\|::\\)")))
          (save-excursion
            (save-restriction
              (unless (org-before-first-heading-p) (org-narrow-to-subtree))
              (goto-char (point-min))
              ;; re-search-forward 自己会前进, 不需要额外的 eobp 守卫
              (while (re-search-forward re nil t)
                (replace-match (concat "\\1" (org-link-escape new) "\\2") t)
                (setq count (1+ count)))))
          (message "Renamed to %s, rewrote %d link(s)" new count)))))

  (defvar my/org-attach-download-dir "~/Downloads/"
    "Where `my/org-attach-latest-download' looks for the newest file.")

  (defun my/org-attach-latest-download ()
    "Attach the newest file in the download directory and insert a link."
    (interactive)
    (let ((file (car (sort
                      (seq-filter
                       (lambda (f)
                         (and (file-regular-p f)
                              (not (string-match-p
                                    "\\.\\(crdownload\\|download\\|part\\|tmp\\)\\'"
                                    f))))
                       (directory-files
                        (expand-file-name my/org-attach-download-dir) t "\\`[^.]"))
                      #'file-newer-than-file-p))))
      (unless file (user-error "The download directory is empty"))
      (when (y-or-n-p (format "Attach %s? " (file-name-nondirectory file)))
        (my/org-attach--insert-link
         (string-remove-prefix "attachment:" (car (org-attach-attach file)))))))

  (defun my/org-attach-file (file)
    "Attach FILE to the current heading and insert a link."
    (interactive "fAttach file: ")
    (my/org-attach--insert-link
     (string-remove-prefix "attachment:" (car (org-attach-attach file)))))

  (defun my/org-attach-xdg-open ()
    "Open an attachment with the desktop default application."
    (interactive)
    (let ((file (expand-file-name (my/org-attach--read "Open: " current-prefix-arg)
                                  (my/org-attach--dir))))
      (call-process "xdg-open" nil 0 nil file)))

  ;;; 5b. 孤儿扫描 (平铺池子没有 per-entry 目录, 「谁引用了它」只能看链接)
  (defvar my/org-attach-orphan-scan-files
    '("inbox.org")
    "Org files scanned for attachment links, relative to `org-directory'.
2026-10-18 退役: notes/things/finance/health/media/read 已并入笔记池
(roam/, 2026-10-24 起 notes/),
这份列表只剩根目录的 inbox(收件箱) —— 其余一律走
`my/org-attach--scan-files' 里的 roam 递归, 加新节点不用再来改这张表。
2026-10-24 晚: 日记取消加密 + 明文化, 引用的附件不再因 GPG 密文被 epa 正则跳过
(历史: 当天白天日记还在 ~/org/journal/ 且全 GPG 密文 → 附件成孤儿, 取消加密后消失)。
2026-11-01: 日记整体迁到 org/journal/ (按年合并), 不在 notes/ 递归扫描内,
故在 `my/org-attach--scan-files' 里单独把 journal/ 递归加回来, 附件链接照常算数。
2026-11-06: 日记合并成根目录单文件 ~/org/journal.org 并恢复 GPG 加密 →
scan-files 改成直接列这个文件; 且 `--linked-names' 里对它有专门的解密读分支,
否则 42 条 attachment: 链接会重新变成「孤儿」误报 (2026-10-24 前的老坑)。")

  (defun my/org-attach--scan-files ()
    "Every Org file that might link into the attachment pool."
    (let (files)
      (dolist (rel my/org-attach-orphan-scan-files)
        (push (expand-file-name rel org-directory) files))
      ;; notes/ 是全库唯一的笔记真源, 递归扫全部节点。
      ;; 注意别把 .retired-*/ 或 _orig-*/ 之类的归档挪进来 —— 它们仍留着
      ;; attachment: 链接, 扫进去会把本该回收的孤儿判成「有人引用」。
      (dolist (f (directory-files-recursively
                  (expand-file-name "notes/" org-directory) "\\.org\\'"))
        (push f files))
      ;; 2026-11-06: 日记合并成根目录单文件 ~/org/journal.org (GPG 密文), 虽不在
      ;; org-roam-directory / agenda 扫描树内, 但 42 条 attachment: 链接仍指向 data/,
      ;; 必须纳入孤儿扫描 —— `--linked-names' 里对它走解密读分支 (见下)。
      (when (file-exists-p my/journal-file)
        (push my/journal-file files))
      ;; 归档出去的条目还留着链接, 不扫会误报
      (dolist (f (file-expand-wildcards
                  (expand-file-name ".archive/*.org" org-directory)))
        (push f files))
      (seq-filter #'file-readable-p (seq-uniq files))))

  (defun my/org-attach--linked-names ()
    "Hash table of every file name mentioned by a file:/attachment: link.
这里只需「有没有出现过」, 不需要出现在哪 —— 所以把候选文件统统拼进一个
临时 buffer 再统一扫, 比逐文件 re-search 快得多。"
    (let ((table (make-hash-table :test #'equal))
          (enc (or (bound-and-true-p epa-file-name-regexp) "\\`\\'")))
      (with-temp-buffer
        (dolist (f (my/org-attach--scan-files))
          (cond
           ;; 日记单文件虽是 GPG 密文, 但必须读 —— 42 条 attachment: 链接在里面。
           ;; epa handler 会在 `insert-file-contents' 时透明解密 (加密子密钥已去
           ;; 口令, 不弹 pinentry)。2026-10-24 前「一律跳过密文」正是附件被
           ;; 误报成孤儿的根源, 这里单独开一个分支把它修掉。
           ((equal f my/journal-file)
            (ignore-errors (insert-file-contents f)))
           ;; 其它匹配 epa 正则的历史密文文件仍然跳过
           ((string-match-p enc f) nil)
           (t (ignore-errors (insert-file-contents f)))))
        (goto-char (point-min))
        (while (re-search-forward
                "\\[\\[\\(?:attachment\\|file\\):\\([^]|]+\\)" nil t)
          (let* ((target (match-string-no-properties 1))
                 ;; data/xxx 与 ../notes/.images/xxx 都取最后一段
                 (name (car (last (split-string target "/")))))
            (unless (string-empty-p name)
              (puthash (org-link-unescape name) t table)))))
      table))

  (defun my/org-attach-list-orphans ()
    "Report files in `org-attach-id-dir' that no Org link refers to.
平铺池子里「哪个文件属于谁」只能靠链接判断; 链接删了、条目被覆盖、或者文件是
旁路进来的(手机 / shell 拷贝, 不走 `org-attach-attach'), 它就永远留在池子里没人
知道 —— 还得跟着 rclone 双向同步到 R2。

注: 2026-11-06 起日记合并成根目录 ~/org/journal.org 单文件并恢复 GPG 加密;
`my/org-attach--scan-files' 直接列它, `--linked-names' 对它走解密读分支,
故 42 条日记附件链接照常被计入, 下面列出的孤儿仍是**真孤儿**。"
    (interactive)
    (let* ((dir   (file-name-as-directory org-attach-id-dir))
           (table (my/org-attach--linked-names))
           (all   (directory-files dir nil "\\`[^.]" t))
           rows total)
      (dolist (f all)
        (unless (gethash f table)
          (let ((size (file-attribute-size
                       (file-attributes (expand-file-name f dir)))))
            (setq total (+ (or total 0) size))
            (push (cons size f) rows))))
      (setq rows (sort rows (lambda (a b) (> (car a) (car b)))))
      (with-current-buffer (get-buffer-create "*org-attach orphans*")
        (let ((inhibit-read-only t))
          (erase-buffer)
          (insert (format ";; %d / %d files in %s are referenced by no org link.\n"
                          (length rows) (length all) dir))
          (insert (format ";; total %s\n" (file-size-human-readable (or total 0))))
          (insert ";; (journal.org is GPG ciphertext since 2026-11-06, decrypted on read\n")
          (insert ";;  in `my/org-attach--linked-names'; orphans below are genuine.)\n\n")
          (dolist (row rows)
            (insert (format "%8s  %s\n"
                            (file-size-human-readable (car row))
                            (expand-file-name (cdr row) dir)))))
        (goto-char (point-min))
        (special-mode)
        (pop-to-buffer (current-buffer)))
      (message "%d orphan(s), %s — see *org-attach orphans*"
               (length rows) (file-size-human-readable (or total 0)))))

  ;;; 6. transient 菜单 (Linux 版, 没有 Quick Look / Finder)
  (after! transient
    (transient-define-prefix my/org-attach-menu ()
      "Attachments of the current heading."
      [["Add"
        ("a" "Pick a file"        my/org-attach-file)
        ("l" "Newest download"    my/org-attach-latest-download)
        ("v" "Clipboard image"    my/org-attach-paste-image)
        ("u" "From URL"           org-attach-url)
        ("b" "Contents of buffer" org-attach-buffer)]
       ;; 不用 org-attach-open / org-attach-delete-one / org-attach-sync:
       ;; 前两个列整个目录 (平铺后是上百个无关文件), sync 的 ATTACH tag 语义
       ;; 在共享池子里没有意义。org-attach-delete-all 由上面的 advice 拦住。
       ["View"
        ("o" "Open"               my/org-attach-open)
        ("O" "xdg-open"           my/org-attach-xdg-open)
        ("f" "Directory in Dired" org-attach-reveal-in-emacs)
        ("i" "Insert link"        my/org-attach-insert-link)]
       ["Organise"
        ("r" "Rename"             my/org-attach-rename)
        ("d" "Delete one"         my/org-attach-delete-one)
        ("s" "Set directory"      org-attach-set-directory)
        ("g" "List orphans"       my/org-attach-list-orphans)
        ("?" "Stock dispatcher"   org-attach)]])))
#+end_src

** CJK emphasis fix (from ljg)
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(after! org
  ;; @Eli 帮忙写的解决标记符号前后空格问题的代码
  (setq org-emphasis-regexp-components '("-[:space:]('\"{[:nonascii:]"
                                         "-[:space:].,:!?;'\")}\\[[:nonascii:]"
                                         "[:space:]"
                                         "."
                                         1))
  (setq org-match-substring-regexp
        (concat
         "\\([0-9a-zA-Zα-γΑ-Ω]\\)\\([_^]\\)\\("
         "\\(?:" (org-create-multibrace-regexp "{" "}" org-match-sexp-depth) "\\)"
         "\\|"
         "\\(?:" (org-create-multibrace-regexp "(" ")" org-match-sexp-depth) "\\)"
         "\\|"
         "\\(?:\\*\\|[+-]?[[:alnum:].,\\]*[[:alnum:]]\\)\\)"))
  (org-set-emph-re 'org-emphasis-regexp-components org-emphasis-regexp-components)
  (org-element-update-syntax)

  ;; 标记字符前后空格优化问题
  (defun eli/org-do-emphasis-faces (limit)
    "Run through the buffer and emphasize strings."
    (let ((quick-re (format "\\([%s]\\|^\\)\\([~=*/_+]\\)"
                            (car org-emphasis-regexp-components))))
      (catch :exit
        (while (re-search-forward quick-re limit t)
          (let* ((marker (match-string 2))
                 (verbatim? (member marker '("~" "="))))
            (when (save-excursion
                    (goto-char (match-beginning 0))
                    (and
                     (not (save-excursion
                            (forward-char 1)
                            (get-pos-property (point) 'org-emphasis)))
                     (not (org-match-line
                           "^[    ]*:\\(\\(?:\\w\\|[-_]\\)+\\):[      ]*"))
                     (not (and (equal marker "+")
                               (org-match-line
                                "[ \t]*\\(|[-+]+|?\\|\\+[-+]+\\+\\)[ \t]*$")))
                     (not (and (equal marker "*")
                               (save-excursion
                                 (forward-char)
                                 (skip-chars-backward "*")
                                 (looking-at-p org-outline-regexp-bol))))
                     (looking-at (if verbatim? org-verbatim-re org-emph-re))
                     (not (string-match-p org-element-paragraph-separate
                                          (match-string 2)))
                     (not (and (save-match-data (org-match-line "[ \t]*|"))
                               (string-match-p "|" (match-string 4))))))
              (pcase-let ((`(,_ ,face ,_) (assoc marker org-emphasis-alist))
                          (m (if org-hide-emphasis-markers 4 2)))
                (font-lock-prepend-text-property
                 (match-beginning m) (match-end m) 'face face)
                (when verbatim?
                  (org-remove-flyspell-overlays-in
                   (match-beginning 0) (match-end 0))
                  (when (and (org-fold-core-folding-spec-p 'org-link)
                             (org-fold-core-folding-spec-p 'org-link-description))
                    (org-fold-region (match-beginning 0) (match-end 0) nil 'org-link)
                    (org-fold-region (match-beginning 0) (match-end 0) nil 'org-link-description))
                  (remove-text-properties (match-beginning 2) (match-end 2)
                                          '(display t invisible t intangible t)))
                (add-text-properties (match-beginning 2) (match-end 2)
                                     '(font-lock-multiline t org-emphasis t))
                (when (and org-hide-emphasis-markers
                           (not (org-at-comment-p)))
                  (add-text-properties (match-end 4) (match-beginning 5)
                                       '(invisible t))
                  (add-text-properties (match-beginning 3) (match-end 3)
                                       '(invisible t)))
                (throw :exit t))))))))

  (advice-add #'org-do-emphasis-faces :override #'eli/org-do-emphasis-faces)

  (defun eli/org-element--parse-generic-emphasis (mark type)
    "Parse emphasis object at point, if any."
    (save-excursion
      (let ((origin (point)))
        (unless (bolp) (forward-char -1))
        (let ((opening-re
               (rx-to-string
                `(seq (or line-start (any space ?- ?\( ?' ?\" ?\{ nonascii))
                  ,mark
                  (not space)))))
          (when (looking-at opening-re)
            (goto-char (1+ origin))
            (let ((closing-re
                   (rx-to-string
                    `(seq
                      (not space)
                      (group ,mark)
                      (or (any space ?- ?. ?, ?\; ?: ?! ?? ?' ?\" ?\) ?\} ?\\ ?\[
                               nonascii)
                          line-end)))))
              (when (re-search-forward closing-re nil t)
                (let ((closing (match-end 1)))
                  (goto-char closing)
                  (let* ((post-blank (skip-chars-forward " \t"))
                         (contents-begin (1+ origin))
                         (contents-end (1- closing)))
                    (list type
                          (append
                           (list :begin origin
                                 :end (point)
                                 :post-blank post-blank)
                           (if (memq type '(code verbatim))
                               (list :value
                                     (and (memq type '(code verbatim))
                                          (buffer-substring
                                           contents-begin contents-end)))
                             (list :contents-begin contents-begin
                                   :contents-end contents-end)))))))))))))

  (advice-add #'org-element--parse-generic-emphasis :override #'eli/org-element--parse-generic-emphasis))
#+end_src

** Agenda
2026-10-26 起**彻底原生**: 每个视图 = 1 个 agenda block (有时间戳的条目) +
`my/agenda-state-blocks' 返回的 4 个 todo block (按 TODO 关键字分: NEXT / PROG /
WAIT / 裸 TODO), 每个 block 自带 overriding-header。没有任何外部包参与分组。

历史: 2026-10-23 起 org-super-agenda 被 :disable t, 配置里的 group 一度沦为惰性
数据 (视图退化成两个平铺 block); 2026-10-26 连包带 group defvar / use-package!
块 / face 一起删干净, 分组改用原生多 block 实现 —— 少一层依赖, 也算把这半年
"分组时有时无" 的账结了。
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(after! org
  (setq org-agenda-inhibit-startup t
        org-agenda-tags-column 'auto
        ;; ⚠ 2026-11-05 收敛: agenda 只扫 inbox.org + .calendar/ 两个来源。
        ;; 历史: 10-18 ~ 11-02 一直是「inbox + .calendar + notes/ 递归(内联排除
        ;; 记录库/日记)」; 11-02/11-03 各域合并进根目录单文件后 notes/ 只剩 6 篇
        ;; 残留, 其中 5 篇被排除判据滤掉, 实际只有 zettel 1 篇进 agenda ——
        ;; Sean 决定连这 1 篇也去掉, agenda 回归「待办 + 日历」。
        ;; ⚠ 必须是**字面值**, 别调用后面才定义的 `my/notes-*' 谓词:
        ;; `org-agenda-files' 在**加载期**求值, 调用必报 void-function。
        ;; ⚠ 与搜索通道分家: `my/org-ql-files' / consult-notes 仍按全库节点扫。
        ;; ⚠ `my/org-gcal-dedup-after-fetch' 用 `(org-agenda-files t)' 收集
        ;; :entry-id: 去重, 范围随之缩小 (4633 行); 当前 notes/ 下 0 个
        ;; :entry-id:, 无实际影响。若日后日历条目重复拉取, 给那个函数补
        ;; `my/org-ql-files' 作额外来源, 别把文件塞回 agenda。
        org-agenda-files '("~/org/inbox.org" "~/org/.calendar")
        org-default-notes-file "~/org/inbox.org")

  ;; Archive
  (setq org-archive-location
        (concat (expand-file-name ".archive/" org-directory)
                "%s_archive.org::"))

  ;; Time grid
  (setq org-agenda-use-time-grid t
        org-agenda-show-current-time-in-grid t
        org-agenda-time-grid
        '((daily today)
          (600 800 1000 1200 1400 1600 1800 2000 2200)
          " ┄┄┄┄┄ " "┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄")
        org-agenda-current-time-string
        "◀── now ─────────────────────")

  ;; Hide redundant tags in agenda
  (setq org-agenda-hide-tags-regexp "personal\\|habit\\|index")

  (setq org-stuck-projects '("" nil nil ""))

  (defun my/skip-habit ()
    "Skip entries with :STYLE: habit."
    (let ((subtree-end (save-excursion (org-end-of-subtree t))))
      (when (string= (org-entry-get nil "STYLE") "habit")
        subtree-end)))

  ;; ---- 视图分块助手 ----
  ;; 2026-10-26: org-super-agenda 彻底移除后, 分组只能靠**原生多 block** 实现:
  ;; 一个 TODO 关键字一个 `todo' block, 各自带 overriding-header。
  ;; ⚠ 传的是**TODO 关键字字符串** (`org-todo-list' 的 ARG), 不是 tags 搜索串:
  ;;   写 "NEXT" 对, 写 TODO="NEXT" 一个都匹配不到 (实测 2026-10-26)。
  ;; ⚠ TODO 关键字集合以 `org-todo-keywords' 为准 (TODO NEXT PROG WAIT | DONE FAIL),
  ;;   这里没有 WAITING / HOLD —— 那是旧 super-agenda group 里的臆造字。
  ;;   多个关键字用竖线: "WAIT|WAITING"。
  ;; ⚠ 每个 block 都必须保留 `org-agenda-overriding-header', 否则原生 agenda 会把
  ;;   相邻 block 的内容糊成一坨看不出边界。
  (defun my/agenda-state-blocks ()
    "按 TODO 状态分块的无时间戳任务 block 列表。
给 `org-agenda-custom-commands' 用: 一个视图 = agenda block + 这里返回的若干个
todo block。**顺序是 Sean 定的**: 进行中 → 下一步 → 待安排 → 等待
(先看手上正在做的, 再挑下一个, 兜底的裸 TODO 居中, 阻塞的排最后)。"
    '((todo "PROG"
            ((org-agenda-overriding-header "🚧 进行中")
             (org-agenda-skip-function 'my/skip-habit)))
      (todo "NEXT"
            ((org-agenda-overriding-header "⚡ Next Actions")
             (org-agenda-skip-function 'my/skip-habit)))
      (todo "TODO"
            ((org-agenda-overriding-header "📋 待安排")
             (org-agenda-skip-function 'my/skip-habit)))
      (todo "WAIT"
            ((org-agenda-overriding-header "⏳ Waiting")
             (org-agenda-skip-function 'my/skip-habit)))))

  ;; ---- 视图定义 ----
  ;; 2026-10-26: 彻底原生化。一个视图 = 1 个 agenda block (处理有时间戳的条目)
  ;; + `my/agenda-state-blocks' 返回的 4 个 todo block (按 TODO 关键字分)。
  ;; 没有任何外部包参与分组, 所见即 org 自带行为。
  ;; 用反引号 ` + ,@ 把分块列表拼进每个视图, 改 `my/agenda-state-blocks'
  ;; 三个视图一起生效 (改完要重新求值这个 setq 或重启 Emacs)。

  (setq org-agenda-custom-commands
        `(("d" "Daily · 今日聚焦"
           ((agenda ""
                    ((org-agenda-span 'day)
                     (org-agenda-start-day nil)
                     (org-deadline-warning-days 3)
                     (org-agenda-skip-scheduled-if-done t)
                     (org-agenda-skip-deadline-if-done t)
                     (org-agenda-overriding-header "── 今日日程 ──")))
            ,@(my/agenda-state-blocks)))

          ("w" "Weekly · 周视图"
           ((agenda ""
                    ((org-agenda-span 'week)
                     (org-agenda-start-on-weekday 1)        ; 周一起
                     ;; ⚠ 2026-11-04: 必须显式置 nil, 否则 doom 的全局
                     ;;   org-agenda-start-day = "-3d" 会生效。org-agenda.el 的
                     ;;   起点算法: sd = (or start-day org-agenda-start-day) = 今天-3天
                     ;;   → 再按 start-on-weekday 回退到最近的周一。三者叠加后
                     ;;   今天(周三)得到的是**上周一**, agenda 显示成上一周。
                     ;;   判据: 本周视图必须覆盖到今天及未来。
                     (org-agenda-start-day nil)
                     (org-deadline-warning-days 7)
                     (org-habit-show-habits nil)            ; 周视图不显示习惯网格
                     (org-agenda-overriding-header "── 本周日程 ──")))
            ,@(my/agenda-state-blocks)))

          ("g" "GTD · 周回顾"
           ((agenda ""
                    ((org-agenda-span 'day)
                     (org-agenda-start-day nil)
                     (org-deadline-warning-days 7)
                     (org-agenda-overriding-header "── 今日日程 / 本周到期 ──")))
            ,@(my/agenda-state-blocks)))

          ;; 全局搜索入口, 不分组方便临时翻
          ("A" "All TODOs (no grouping)"
           ((alltodo ""
                     ((org-agenda-overriding-header "All Open TODOs")
                      (org-agenda-skip-function 'my/skip-habit))))))))
#+end_src

** Agenda 配色（明暗自适应）

*更正一条旧结论*: 之前这里写「doom-themes 里没有 =org-agenda-*= 的 face 定义」是**错的**。
=doom-themes-base.el:1217-1229= 定义了全套, 其中:

#+begin_example
(org-time-grid :foreground comments)
#+end_example

这才是时间线看不见的真根因 —— =doom-omarchy= 的 =comments= 取
=(if doom-omarchy-brighter-comments base5 base1)=, 默认 =base1 = #4d5063=,
在 =#1e1e2e= 底上对比度只有 **2.06:1**; 而 =org-agenda-current-time= 继承
=org-time-grid=, 那条「◀── now ──」指示线跟着一起隐形了。

（顺带一提: 旧注释里说的 X11 =LightGoldenrod= 那是**非 doom 主题**才走到的默认值,
切到 doom 家族后压根不会用到。）

下面按主题明暗重算: 深底把语义色往前景提亮, 浅底往背景压暗 —— =alpha= 在两种
情况下都表示「要多接近主题主色」, 语义对称, 不用为深浅各维护一套色值。

注意: 仍然必须用**带主题参数**的方式（这里直接用 =custom-theme-set-faces= +
=doom-theme= 变量）, 不能用裸 =custom-set-faces!= 。后者写进 =user= theme,
对**所有**主题生效, 切回浅色主题时这套配色会糊死白底。
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(after! org
  (defun my/org-agenda-faces-apply ()
    "重算 agenda 配色。挂 `doom-load-theme-hook', 换主题时自动跟随。

时间网格是背景性元素, 够看就行 (目标 ~3:1); 当前时间指示线要能一眼扫到
(目标 ~6:1); 已完成项收敛, 别抢视线。"
    (let* ((bg (my/theme-face-color :background))
           (fg (my/theme-face-color :foreground))
           (dark-p (< (my/color-luminance bg) 0.5))
           ;; 同上: 先归到 9:1 再朝背景混, alpha = 离背景多远。
           ;; (原来朝前景混, 浅底上 alpha 越低反而越显眼, 语义是反的。)
           (mix (lambda (hue alpha)
                  (doom-blend (if (eq hue 'fg)
                                  fg
                                (my/color-ensure-contrast (my/theme-hue hue) bg 9.0))
                              bg
                              alpha)))
           (subtle (lambda (alpha) (doom-blend fg bg alpha))))
      (custom-theme-set-faces
       doom-theme
       ;; 时间网格 "8:00 ┄┄┄┄┄"   ← comments(base1) 2.06:1 → ~3.1:1
       `(org-time-grid ((t (:foreground ,(funcall subtle (if dark-p 0.42 0.55))))))
       ;; 当前时间指示线 "◀── now ──"   ← 原本继承 org-time-grid 一起隐形
       `(org-agenda-current-time ((t (:foreground ,(funcall mix 'red 0.90) :weight bold))))
       ;; 日期行
       `(org-agenda-date ((t (:foreground ,(funcall mix 'violet 0.85) :weight bold))))
       `(org-agenda-date-today ((t (:foreground ,(funcall mix 'yellow 0.95) :weight bold))))
       `(org-agenda-date-weekend ((t (:foreground ,(funcall mix 'violet 0.60) :weight bold))))
       `(org-agenda-date-weekend-today ((t (:foreground ,(funcall mix 'yellow 0.95) :weight bold))))
       ;; 结构头 "Day-agenda (W39):"
       `(org-agenda-structure ((t (:foreground ,fg :weight bold))))
       ;; Clocked 行（开 log mode 后会大量出现）
       `(org-agenda-clocking ((t (:foreground ,(funcall mix 'cyan 0.85)))))
       ;; 已完成 —— 收敛
       `(org-agenda-done ((t (:foreground ,(funcall mix 'green 0.42)))))
       ;; Scheduled / Deadline 前缀
       `(org-scheduled ((t (:foreground ,fg))))
       `(org-scheduled-today ((t (:foreground ,(funcall mix 'blue 0.80) :weight bold))))
       `(org-scheduled-previously ((t (:foreground ,(funcall mix 'red 0.90) :weight bold))))
       `(org-upcoming-deadline ((t (:foreground ,(funcall mix 'orange 0.90) :weight bold))))
       ;; Deadline 警告前缀
       `(org-warning ((t (:foreground ,(funcall mix 'red 0.90) :weight bold)))))))

  (add-hook 'doom-load-theme-hook #'my/org-agenda-faces-apply))
#+end_src

** Capture helpers
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(after! org
  ;; ---- Append Note helper ----
  ;; 2026-10-16: append-note.org 已并入 inbox.org (内容在「速记流」heading 下),
  ;; 速记现在直接往 inbox 追加裸 heading, 不再需要日期分隔符与跳转函数。
  (defun my/append-note-open-at-end-h ()
    "打开 inbox.org 时把光标放到末尾 (速记流的追加位置)。"
    (when (and buffer-file-name
               (file-equal-p buffer-file-name
                             (expand-file-name "~/org/inbox.org")))
      (goto-char (point-max))))

  (add-hook 'find-file-hook #'my/append-note-open-at-end-h t)

  ;; ---- Habit capture helper ----
  (defun my/org-capture-habit ()
    "Generate a capture template for a habit."
    (let* ((name (read-string "Habit 名称: "))
           (raw  (read-string "提醒时间 (HH:MM): "))
           (repeat (completing-read "重复周期: "
                                    '(".+1d  — 每天（从完成日起）"
                                      ".+2d  — 每2天"
                                      ".+1w  — 每周"
                                      ".+2w  — 每2周"
                                      ".+1m  — 每月"
                                      "++1d  — 每天（固定日期）"
                                      "++1w  — 每周（固定星期）"
                                      ".+1d/2d — 每天，最多隔2天"
                                      ".+1d/3d — 每天，最多隔3天")
                                    nil t))
           (repeat-val (car (split-string repeat " ")))
           (parts (split-string raw ":"))
           (hour (string-to-number (nth 0 parts)))
           (min  (string-to-number (nth 1 parts)))
           (time (format "%02d:%02d" hour min))
           (today (format-time-string "%Y-%m-%d %a"))
           (end-min (+ min 5))
           (end-hour (+ hour (/ end-min 60)))
           (end-time (format "%02d:%02d" end-hour (% end-min 60))))
      (format "* TODO %s\nSCHEDULED: <%s %s %s>\n:PROPERTIES:\n:STYLE:    habit\n:calendar-id: yuanxiang424@gmail.com\n:END:\n:org-gcal:\n<%s %s-%s>\n:END:\n"
              name today time repeat-val today time end-time))))
#+end_src

** Capture target helpers (web article / protocol note)
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(defun my/capture-web-article-target ()
  "Target function for org-capture: reference note from clipboard URL."
  (let* ((url (string-trim (current-kill 0 t)))
         (title (or (ignore-errors
                      (with-temp-buffer
                        (url-insert-file-contents url)
                        (goto-char (point-min))
                        (when (re-search-forward "<title>\\([^<]*\\)</title>" nil t)
                          (string-trim (match-string 1)))))
                    (read-string "Title: ")))
         (id (format-time-string "%Y%m%dT%H%M%S"))
         (slug (replace-regexp-in-string "[^a-zA-Z0-9一-鿿]+" "-"
                                         (downcase (string-trim title)) t t))
         (slug (replace-regexp-in-string "^-\\|-$" "" slug))
         (file (expand-file-name (format "notes/%s--%s__read.org" id slug) org-directory)))
    (set-buffer (org-capture-target-buffer file))
    (when (= (buffer-size) 0)
      (insert (format "#+title: %s\n#+date: [%s]\n#+filetags: :read:\n#+identifier: %s\n\n* Source\n%s\n\n* Summary\n\n* My Notes\n"
                      title (format-time-string "%Y-%m-%d %a") id url)))
    (goto-char (point-max))
    (or (re-search-backward "^\\* My Notes" nil t) (goto-char (point-max)))
    (forward-line 1)))

#+end_src

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(after! org
  ;; 2026-10-23: `我想读的书单' 一分为四 (网文/小说/非虚构/理财各自成 file-per-node)。
  ;; 定位书单桶文件**不要写死 roam 文件名** —— 2026-10-21 elfeed 事故定的铁律:
  ;; 全库重命名后写死的路径会静默失效。这里是「已知路径优先 → roam db 按节点 ID
  ;; 反查 → 报错」三级: 文件被改名 / 挪走之后自动从 db 捞回来, db 也没有就响出来。
  (defun my/notes-bucket-file (id fallback)
    "返回书单桶文件的绝对路径: 先看 FALLBACK, 再按 roam 节点 ID 反查, 都没有就报错。"
    (let ((fb (expand-file-name fallback)))
      (cond ((file-exists-p fb) fb)
            ;; 2026-10-24: org-roam 退役, `org-roam-node-from-id' 换成 org 原生
            ;; `org-id-find' —— 返回 (FILE . POSITION), 取 car 即文件。
            ;; 密文日记也认: epa 的 file-name-handler 让 org-id 透明解密。
            ((let ((loc (ignore-errors (org-id-find id))))
               (and loc (car loc))))
            (t (error "roam: 找不到书单桶 %s —— %s 不存在, db 里也没有该节点" id fallback)))))

  (setq org-capture-templates
        '(          ("a" "Append Note" entry (file "~/org/inbox.org")
           "* %?"
           :empty-lines 1 :jump-to-captured t)
          ("i" "Inbox" entry (file "~/org/inbox.org")
           "* %^{标题}  %^g\n:PROPERTIES:\n:CREATED: %U\n:END:\n\n%?"
           :empty-lines 1 :jump-to-captured t)
          ("t" "Task" entry (file "~/org/inbox.org")
           "* TODO %?\n:PROPERTIES:\n:CREATED: %U\n:END:\n" :empty-lines 1)
          ;; 2026-10-25 改: 不再插 `* HH:MM' 时间戳标题 (Sean: 「感觉没啥用」)。
          ;; 日记是一天一文件, 文件名 ID 已经带日期, 条目先后由文件里的顺序表达,
          ;; 时间戳只是噪音。现在光标直接停在标题行 (`* %?'): 打什么就是标题,
          ;; 回车写正文, 一个字不打就是空标题 —— 空 heading 仍把一天的条目分隔开,
          ;; 不会退化成一坨平铺文本 (平铺还会被算进上一条 heading 的内容里)。
          ("j" "Journal" entry
           (function my/journal-capture-target)
           "* %<%Y-%m-%d %a> %?\n:PROPERTIES:\n:ID:       %(org-id-new)\n:END:\n"
           :empty-lines 1 :jump-to-captured t)
          ("m" "Media")
          ;; 2026-11-02 改: media 域合并进根目录单文件 ~/org/media.org
          ;; (11 篇 → 11 个一级 heading), notes/media/ 目录已不存在。
          ;; 合并后结构是 `* 电影记录 > ** 想看 > *** 片名` —— 类别成了文件里的
          ;; 一级 heading, 桶是二级, 条目是三级。
          ;;   → file+olp 的目标文件改成 ~/org/media.org, olp 补上**类别一级**
          ;;     (原文件里类别是文件名, olp 只剩一级桶名)。
          ;;   → 模板层级从 `**' 回到 `***' (原来拆文件时降过一级, 现在补回来)。
          ;;   1) 状态写 :状态: 属性, 不写 TODO 关键字 —— P3 已把 media 的全部
          ;;      伪 TODO 迁成属性 (未开始/已完成/进行中/已放弃); 模板继续写
          ;;      `*** TODO 片名' 会让新条目用第二套状态机制, agenda 与抽屉
          ;;      里的 :状态: 互不认账。
          ;;   2) 必须带 :ID: —— 没有 ID 的 heading 不会进 org-roam 的 nodes 表,
          ;;      capture 出来的片子搜不到、也连不上 backlinks。
          ("mm" "电影 · 想看" entry
           (file+olp "~/org/media.org" "电影记录" "想看")
           "*** %^{片名}\n:PROPERTIES:\n:ID:       %(org-id-new)\n:状态: 未开始\n:添加时间: %U\n:END:\n%?"
           :empty-lines 1 :jump-to-captured t)
          ("mM" "电影 · 看完" entry
           (file+olp "~/org/media.org" "电影记录" "看完")
           "*** %^{片名}\n:PROPERTIES:\n:ID:       %(org-id-new)\n:状态: 已完成\n:完成日期: %<[%Y-%m-%d]>\n:END:\n%?"
           :empty-lines 1 :jump-to-captured t)
          ("mt" "电视剧 · 想看" entry
           (file+olp "~/org/media.org" "电视剧记录" "想看")
           "*** %^{片名}\n:PROPERTIES:\n:ID:       %(org-id-new)\n:状态: 未开始\n:添加时间: %U\n:END:\n%?"
           :empty-lines 1 :jump-to-captured t)
          ("mT" "电视剧 · 看完" entry
           (file+olp "~/org/media.org" "电视剧记录" "看完")
           "*** %^{片名}\n:PROPERTIES:\n:ID:       %(org-id-new)\n:状态: 已完成\n:完成日期: %<[%Y-%m-%d]>\n:END:\n%?"
           :empty-lines 1 :jump-to-captured t)
          ("ma" "动漫 · 想看" entry
           (file+olp "~/org/media.org" "动漫记录" "想看")
           "*** %^{片名}\n:PROPERTIES:\n:ID:       %(org-id-new)\n:状态: 未开始\n:添加时间: %U\n:END:\n%?"
           :empty-lines 1 :jump-to-captured t)
          ("mA" "动漫 · 看完" entry
           (file+olp "~/org/media.org" "动漫记录" "看完")
           "*** %^{片名}\n:PROPERTIES:\n:ID:       %(org-id-new)\n:状态: 已完成\n:完成日期: %<[%Y-%m-%d]>\n:END:\n%?"
           :empty-lines 1 :jump-to-captured t)
          ("b" "Books")
          ;; 2026-10-19 二改: read.org 拆成 roam/read/ 下 17 个文件。原 olp
          ;; ("书" "待阅读" / "阅读中") 本来就指向不存在的标题 (这套模板从没被用过,
          ;; 全库 0 个 :来源:-by-capture 条目), 拆完后连文件都没了。
          ;; 2026-10-23 三改: `我想读的书单' 本身又拆成 4 个文件, 桶抬升成文件级
          ;; 节点 (`20261017210001/2/3/4-*书单.org'), 条目从 `**' 降成 `*'。
          ;; 于是: 模板层级跟着降一级 (** → *), 目标文件也跟着换。
          ;; 桶的选择仍旧只能写死一个 (capture 的 target 在 `%^{类型}' 求值之前
          ;; 就解析完了), 这里默认落 `小说书单'; 要按类型自动分桶就得开 4 个键位,
          ;; 由 Sean 定。
          ("bb" "书 · 待阅读" entry
           (file (lambda ()
                   (my/notes-bucket-file "20261017T210002"
                                            "~/org/notes/read/20261017T210002--小说书单__read.org")))
           "* %^{书名}\n:PROPERTIES:\n:ID:       %(org-id-new)\n:状态: 未开始\n:作者: %^{作者}\n:类型: %^{类型|小说|非虚构|理财|网文|漫画|其他}\n:来源: %^{来源|微信读书|豆瓣|Z-Library|实体书|其他}\n:添加时间: %U\n:END:\n%?"
           :empty-lines 1 :jump-to-captured t)
          ("bB" "书 · 阅读中" entry
           (file (lambda ()
                   (my/notes-bucket-file "20261017T210002"
                                            "~/org/notes/read/20261017T210002--小说书单__read.org")))
           "* %^{书名}\n:PROPERTIES:\n:ID:       %(org-id-new)\n:状态: 在读\n:作者: %^{作者}\n:类型: %^{类型|小说|非虚构|理财|网文|漫画|其他}\n:来源: %^{来源|微信读书|豆瓣|Z-Library|实体书|其他}\n:添加时间: %U\n:END:\n%?"
           :empty-lines 1 :jump-to-captured t)
          ("pl" "Protocol: Read later" entry (file "~/org/inbox.org")
           "* TODO %:annotation\n:PROPERTIES:\n:CREATED: %U\n:END:\n%i\n"
           :immediate-finish t :jump-to-captured t)
          )))
