#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(defun my/eshell-here ()
  "Open a new eshell rooted at the current buffer's directory."
  (interactive)
  (let ((default-directory (or (and buffer-file-name
                                    (file-name-directory buffer-file-name))
                               default-directory)))
    (eshell t)))

(defun eshell/doom (&rest args)
  "Run Doom Emacs CLI from eshell.
Invokes the current Emacs executable in batch mode with the doom script,
identical to what `doom.ps1' does on Windows."
  (let ((doom-script (expand-file-name "~/.emacs.d/bin/doom"))
        (emacs-bin (expand-file-name invocation-name invocation-directory)))
    (if (file-exists-p doom-script)
        (apply #'call-process emacs-bin nil t nil
               "-q" "--no-site-file" "--batch"
               "--load" doom-script "--" "--no-color"
               args)
      (error "Doom script not found at %s" doom-script))))
#+end_src

** Elfeed
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package! elfeed
  :commands elfeed
  :config
  (require 'subr-x)
  (setq elfeed-db-directory (expand-file-name "~/org/.elfeed")
        elfeed-curl-max-connections 4)
  (setq-default elfeed-search-filter "+unread")
  ;; 2026-10-23: 文章窗从底部改到右侧, 实现「左列表 / 右文章」左右分屏。
  ;; 根因不是 elfeed 原生行为, 而是 Doom rss 模块的 popup 规则 ——
  ;; 它在 display-buffer-alist 里把 *elfeed-entry* 钉死在
  ;; display-buffer-below-selected (side bottom)。elfeed 4.x 的显示链路是
  ;; elfeed-show-entry-switch (pop-to-buffer) -> display-buffer -> 命中该规则,
  ;; 所以改 split-width-threshold / elfeed-show-entry-switch 都没用。
  ;; 这里用同名规则覆盖它 (Doom 的 set-popup-rule! 会替换同 regexp 的旧条目)。
  ;; 2026-10-23 装了 elfeed-goodies 后此规则退居二线: goodies 的 setup 会把
  ;; entry-switch 换成 switch-pane (popwin 窗口管理, 不经 display-buffer),
  ;; 实际布局改由 elfeed-goodies/entry-pane-position 决定 (同为 right/0.55)。
  ;; 保留本规则作兜底: 卸载 goodies 时它自动重新接管。
  (set-popup-rule! "*elfeed-entry*" :side 'right :size 0.55 :select t :quit t)
  ;; 避免每次进入 elfeed 都疯狂触发后台更新导致界面跳动。
  ;; 建议手动按 r / gr 更新，或者使用定时器。
  ;; (add-hook 'elfeed-search-mode-hook #'elfeed-update)

  (defun my/elfeed-show-buffer-setup ()
    "Apply local display settings for Elfeed article buffers."
    (setq-local shr-use-fonts nil)
    (setq-local line-spacing 0))

  (defun my/elfeed-select-show-window (&rest _)
    "Keep focus in the Elfeed article pane after show-buffer navigation."
    (when-let ((window (cl-find-if
                        (lambda (window)
                          (with-current-buffer (window-buffer window)
                            (derived-mode-p 'elfeed-show-mode)))
                        (window-list nil 'no-minibuf))))
      (select-window window)))

  (add-hook 'elfeed-show-mode-hook #'my/elfeed-show-buffer-setup)
  (unless (advice-member-p #'my/elfeed-select-show-window 'elfeed-show-next)
    (advice-add 'elfeed-show-next :after #'my/elfeed-select-show-window))
  (unless (advice-member-p #'my/elfeed-select-show-window 'elfeed-show-prev)
    (advice-add 'elfeed-show-prev :after #'my/elfeed-select-show-window))

  ;; Avoid expensive reload/save on exit when Elfeed DB is not initialized.
  (defun my/elfeed-db-save-if-initialized (orig-fn &rest args)
    (cond
     ((null elfeed-db)
      (message "Skip elfeed-db-save: elfeed-db is not initialized in this session."))
     ((not (plistp elfeed-db))
      (message "Skip elfeed-db-save: invalid elfeed-db (%S)." elfeed-db))
     (t
      (apply orig-fn args))))
  (when (advice-member-p #'my/elfeed-db-sanitize-before-save 'elfeed-db-save)
    (advice-remove 'elfeed-db-save #'my/elfeed-db-sanitize-before-save))
  (unless (advice-member-p #'my/elfeed-db-save-if-initialized 'elfeed-db-save)
    (advice-add 'elfeed-db-save :around #'my/elfeed-db-save-if-initialized))
  (remove-hook 'kill-emacs-hook #'elfeed-db-gc-safe)

  (defvar my/elfeed-feed-history nil
    "Minibuffer history for feed selection commands.")

  (defun my/elfeed-current-entry ()
    "Return the current Elfeed entry in search or show buffers."
    (cond
     ((derived-mode-p 'elfeed-show-mode) elfeed-show-entry)
     ((derived-mode-p 'elfeed-search-mode) (elfeed-search-selected :ignore-region))
     (t nil)))

  (defun my/elfeed-feed-display-title (feed)
    (or (elfeed-meta feed :title)
        (elfeed-feed-title feed)
        (elfeed-feed-url feed)
        "Unknown feed"))

  (defun my/elfeed-url-origin (url)
    (when (and url (string-match "\\`\\(https?://[^/]+\\)" url))
      (concat (match-string 1 url) "/")))

  (defun my/elfeed-guess-homepage (feed-url)
    (when feed-url
      (let ((homepage (car (split-string feed-url "[?#]" t))))
        (dolist (pattern '("/index\\.xml/?$"
                           "/atom\\.xml/?$"
                           "/rss\\.xml/?$"
                           "/feed\\.xml/?$"
                           "/feeds?/[^/]+\\(?:\\.xml\\|\\.atom\\|\\.rss\\)?/?$"
                           "/\\(?:feed\\|rss\\|atom\\)/?$"))
          (setq homepage (replace-regexp-in-string pattern "/" homepage t t)))
        (unless (string-match-p "/$" homepage)
          (setq homepage
                (replace-regexp-in-string "/[^/]+\\(?:\\.xml\\|\\.rss\\|\\.atom\\)?$"
                                          "/"
                                          homepage
                                          t
                                          t)))
        (replace-regexp-in-string "/+$" "/" homepage t t))))

  (defun my/elfeed-entry-homepage (entry)
    (when-let* ((feed (elfeed-entry-feed entry))
                (feed-url (elfeed-feed-url feed)))
      (let* ((entry-url (elfeed-entry-link entry))
             (feed-origin (my/elfeed-url-origin feed-url))
             (entry-origin (my/elfeed-url-origin entry-url)))
        (cond
         ((and entry-origin feed-origin (not (string= entry-origin feed-origin)))
          entry-origin)
         (t
          (or (my/elfeed-guess-homepage feed-url)
              entry-origin
              feed-origin))))))

  (defun my/elfeed-browse-homepage ()
    "Open the homepage of the current entry's feed."
    (interactive)
    (if-let ((entry (my/elfeed-current-entry)))
        (if-let ((homepage (my/elfeed-entry-homepage entry)))
            (browse-url homepage)
          (user-error "Could not infer a homepage for this feed"))
      (user-error "No Elfeed entry at point")))

  (defun my/elfeed-toggle-unread ()
    "Toggle the `unread' tag on the current Elfeed entry or entries."
    (interactive)
    (cond
     ((derived-mode-p 'elfeed-search-mode)
      (elfeed-search-toggle-all 'unread))
     ((derived-mode-p 'elfeed-show-mode)
      (if (elfeed-tagged-p 'unread elfeed-show-entry)
          (elfeed-show-untag 'unread)
        (elfeed-show-tag 'unread)))
     (t
      (user-error "Not in an Elfeed buffer"))))

  (defun my/elfeed-feed-candidates ()
    "获取订阅源列表。注意：在文章数极多（几万条以上）时，
`with-elfeed-db-visit` 遍历全库可能会导致 C-c f 出现几秒卡顿。
届时可换用纯列表极速版：
  (let (candidates)
    (elfeed-db-ensure)
    (maphash
     (lambda (url feed)
       (push (cons (format \"%s\\t%s\" (my/elfeed-feed-display-title feed) url) url)
             candidates))
     elfeed-db-feeds)
    (sort candidates (lambda (a b) (string-lessp (car a) (car b)))))"
    (let ((table (make-hash-table :test 'equal)))
      (with-elfeed-db-visit (entry feed)
        (let* ((url (elfeed-feed-url feed))
               (title (my/elfeed-feed-display-title feed))
               (current (or (gethash url table)
                            (list :url url :title title :all 0 :unread 0)))
               (all (1+ (plist-get current :all)))
               (unread (if (elfeed-tagged-p 'unread entry)
                           (1+ (plist-get current :unread))
                         (plist-get current :unread))))
          (puthash url
                   (list :url url :title title :all all :unread unread)
                   table)))
      (sort
       (let (candidates)
         (maphash
          (lambda (url data)
            (push (cons (format "[%d/%d] %s\t%s"
                                (plist-get data :unread)
                                (plist-get data :all)
                                (plist-get data :title)
                                url)
                        url)
                  candidates))
          table)
         candidates)
       (lambda (a b) (string-lessp (car a) (car b))))))

  (defun my/elfeed-read-feed-url ()
    (let* ((all-feeds "[All feeds]")
           (candidates (my/elfeed-feed-candidates))
           (choices (cons all-feeds (mapcar #'car candidates)))
           (choice (if (require 'consult nil t)
                       (consult--read
                        choices
                        :prompt "Feed: "
                        :sort nil
                        :require-match t
                        :history 'my/elfeed-feed-history)
                     (completing-read "Feed: " choices nil t nil 'my/elfeed-feed-history))))
      (unless (equal choice all-feeds)
        (cdr (assoc choice candidates)))))

  (defun my/elfeed-replace-feed-filter (filter feed-url)
    (let ((parts (cl-remove-if (lambda (part) (string-prefix-p "=" part))
                               (split-string (string-trim (or filter "")) "[ \t\n]+" t))))
      (string-join
       (append parts
               (when feed-url
                 (list (concat "=" (regexp-quote feed-url)))))
       " ")))

  (defun my/elfeed-search-filter-by-feed ()
    "Use `consult' or `completing-read' to filter Elfeed by feed."
    (interactive)
    (unless (derived-mode-p 'elfeed-search-mode)
      (user-error "Feed filtering is only available in elfeed-search"))
    (let* ((feed-url (my/elfeed-read-feed-url))
           (new-filter (my/elfeed-replace-feed-filter elfeed-search-filter feed-url)))
      (elfeed-search-set-filter new-filter)
      (message (if feed-url
                   "Filtered Elfeed to %s"
                 "Cleared feed-specific filter")
               (or feed-url "all feeds"))))

  ;; mpv integration
  (defun my/elfeed-play-with-mpv ()
    "Play the current elfeed entry with mpv."
    (interactive)
    (let ((link (if (derived-mode-p 'elfeed-show-mode)
                    (elfeed-entry-link elfeed-show-entry)
                  (let ((entries (elfeed-search-selected)))
                    (when entries
                      (elfeed-entry-link (car entries)))))))
      (if link
          (progn
            (message "Starting mpv for %s..." link)
            (start-process "elfeed-mpv" nil "mpv" link)
            (when (derived-mode-p 'elfeed-search-mode)
              (elfeed-search-untag-all-unread)))
        (message "No link found."))))

  ;; 2026-10-29 加: C-j/C-k 翻上一篇/下一篇。
  ;; ⚠ 必须绑 elfeed-goodies/split-show-* 而不是原生 elfeed-show-next/-prev —— evil 把
  ;;   elfeed 原生 n/p 全吞了 (实测: search 里 n=evil-ex-search-next, p=evil-paste-after;
  ;;   show 里 n/p 同样被遮), evil-collection 只补了 `]]'/`[[' 且仅限 show buffer。
  ;;   goodies 版一次干两件事 (左列表挪一行 + 重绘右 pane), 所以左右 pane 通用。
  (map! :map elfeed-search-mode-map
        :desc "Filter by feed"      "C-c f" #'my/elfeed-search-filter-by-feed
        :desc "Browse feed homepage" "B" #'my/elfeed-browse-homepage
        :desc "Toggle unread"       "R" #'my/elfeed-toggle-unread
        :desc "Play with mpv"       "v" #'my/elfeed-play-with-mpv
        :desc "Next entry"          "C-j" #'elfeed-goodies/split-show-next
        :desc "Previous entry"      "C-k" #'elfeed-goodies/split-show-prev)
  (map! :map elfeed-show-mode-map
        :desc "Browse feed homepage" "B" #'my/elfeed-browse-homepage
        :desc "Toggle unread"       "R" #'my/elfeed-toggle-unread
        :desc "Play with mpv"       "v" #'my/elfeed-play-with-mpv
        :desc "Next entry"          "C-j" #'elfeed-goodies/split-show-next
        :desc "Previous entry"      "C-k" #'elfeed-goodies/split-show-prev))

(use-package! elfeed-org
  :after elfeed
  :config
  (elfeed-org)
  ;; 2026-10-18 退役: notes.org 已并入 roam, elfeed 订阅独立成 roam 节点
  ;; (roam 节点 ID 20260101T000013, 84 条订阅, 逐条 diff 过与退役前一一对应 ——
  ;;  迁移时漏过一条 `***` 跳级的播客 feed, 已补回)。
  ;; elfeed-org 只认「标题以 URL 开头」的 heading; 全库 84 条全部在这一个文件里,
  ;; 所以直接指向 .src/ 下的它 (media/read 也有 URL, 但我们现在只扫 .src/, 不会误收)。
  ;; 注意: 别在其他 roam 节点里写「标题以 http 开头」的普通笔记, 会被当成订阅源。
  ;;
  ;; ⚠ 2026-10-21 修复「M-x elfeed 打不开」:
  ;;   这里原本写死文件名 `20260101T000013--elfeed-subscriptions.org`, 但 10-19 那次
  ;;   全库 roam 重命名(去掉 `T` 分隔符、`--` 改 `_`、标题空格改下划线)之后, 实际文件
  ;;   已经是 `20260101000013-elfeed_subscriptions.org`。写死的路径随之失效, 而
  ;;   `rmh-elfeed-org-check-configuration-file` 对不存在的文件是直接 (error ...),
  ;;   它又被 `rmh-elfeed-org-process-advice` 挂在 `elfeed` 的 :before 上 ——
  ;;   结果就是**每次 M-x elfeed 都在启动前抛错, 界面根本出不来**。
  ;;   改成按文件名模式在 notes/ 下查, 不再依赖具体文件名, 下次重命名不会再炸。
  (defun my/elfeed-org-subscription-file ()
    "Locate the single org file that holds elfeed subscriptions.
Matched by filename pattern under `org-directory'/.elfeed, so a rename
cannot break elfeed again."
    (or (car (directory-files-recursively
              (expand-file-name ".elfeed" org-directory)
              ;; 2026-11-06 二次迁移: 订阅文件 .src/ -> .elfeed/（与 elfeed-db-directory
              ;;   同处，配置数据跟运行时数据放一起）。glob 基目录 misc -> .src -> .elfeed；
              ;;   递归会进 data/ 子目录，但正则只认含 elfeed+subscription 的 .org,
              ;;   不会误收 db 文件。坚果云 pull 只管 inbox.org + 空 SHARED_FILES、
              ;;   R2 只管 data/，都碰不到 .elfeed/ 下的 .org，不会被覆盖。
              ;; 2026-10-27 改: 订阅文件从 notes/software/ 移到顶层 misc/ (它不属于
              ;;   知识笔记, 归入 ~/org/misc/ 收容处, 见 AGENTS.md v7)。glob 基目录从
              ;;   notes 换成 misc; misc/ 里仅 3 个文件, 正则只命中 elfeed 这一个, 无误收。
              ;; 2026-10-24 改: 正则原来写作 `elfeed.*subscriptions?\.org\'',
              ;; 要求 `subscriptions' 后面**紧跟** .org。denote 迁移后文件名变成
              ;; `<ID>--elfeed_subscriptions__emacs.org', 中间插了个 __keywords 段,
              ;; 旧正则匹配不到 -> 退回兜底路径 -> 而兜底路径也随改名失效 ->
              ;; `rmh-elfeed-org-check-configuration-file' 直接 error, 挂在 elfeed
              ;; 的 :before advice 上 => **M-x elfeed 打不开** (复刻 10-21 事故)。
              ;; 放宽成 `subscription.*\.org\'', keywords 段插在中间也认。
              "elfeed.*subscription.*\\.org\\'"))
        ;; 2026-11-06 改: 兜底换成 .elfeed/ 下的**真实文件名**。原兜底写的是
        ;;   `misc/20260101T000013--elfeed_subscriptions__emacs.org', 该文件从来
        ;;   不存在(实际叫 elfeed_subscriptions.org), 一旦 glob 扑空就会喂给
        ;;   rmh-elfeed-org-check-configuration-file 一个不存在的文件 -> error
        ;;   -> M-x elfeed 打不开。改成真实路径, 兜底才真的兜得住。
        ;; 上面那个 glob 递归扫 .elfeed/, 命中就用不到它; 这行只是「万一 glob 扑空
        ;; (比如文件改名成不叫 elfeed_subscriptions 了) 的兜底」。
        (expand-file-name
         ".elfeed/elfeed_subscriptions.org"
         org-directory)))
  (setq rmh-elfeed-org-files (list (my/elfeed-org-subscription-file)))
  ;; 2026-10-25 修: elfeed-org 的 ignore tag 默认是 "ignore", 而订阅文件里一律用
  ;; `:@skip:' —— 两边从没对上过, 所有 @skip feed 一直在被抓取 (elfeed DB 里躺着
  ;; V2EX / reddit 等 @skip feed 即证据, 2026-10-25 实测 84 条全进 elfeed-feeds)。
  ;; 这一行让 `:@skip:' 真正生效: 失效 feed 打 `:@skip:' + 注释行标记后,
  ;; elfeed-org 不再把它加进 elfeed-feeds (README 的 ignore tag 是可配置的)。
  (setq rmh-elfeed-org-ignore-tag "@skip"))

;; ⚠ 2026-10-23 装 elfeed-goodies 时连修两处 (实测确认, 非推测):
;; 1) **必须显式调用 (elfeed-goodies/setup)**。函数名是 elfeed-goodies/setup
;;    (不是 elfeed-goodies —— 那样调用会 void-function); setup 带 ;;;###autoload,
;;    不调用则 elfeed-show-entry-switch / header 绘制 / n p 键位全部不生效,
;;    只 setq 变量 = 装了个空壳。
;; 2) **必须用 use-package! 而不是 after!**。after! 只是 with-eval-after-load
;;    包装, 不产生加载行为; 没有 use-package!/require 触发, 这个包永远不加载,
;;    整段配置静默失效。:after elfeed 保证 setup 跑在 elfeed-show-entry 之前
;;    (setup 设 entry-switch, RET 才用到), 同时保持懒加载不拖慢启动。
;; 布局叠加说明: setup 会把 entry-switch 换成 elfeed-goodies/switch-pane (走 popwin),
;; 上面 Elfeed 块的 set-popup-rule! 退为兜底 —— goodies 卸载时它重新接管。
;; 两者的方向一致 (right / 0.55), 实测左右分屏不变。
;; ⚠ **绝不要开 popwin-mode**: 它会重设 display-buffer-function 全局抢走所有
;; display-buffer 调用, Doom 的几十条 popup 规则 (*elfeed-entry*/*org-roam*/
;; capture 等) 全部失效。elfeed-goodies 的 split 路径不依赖 popwin-mode,
;; switch-pane 直接调 popwin:popup-buffer 建窗, 不开也能用 (2026-10-23 实测)。
(use-package! elfeed-goodies
  :after elfeed
  :config
  (setq elfeed-goodies/entry-pane-position 'right
        elfeed-goodies/entry-pane-size 0.55
        elfeed-goodies/switch-to-entry t
        elfeed-goodies/feed-source-column-width 28
        elfeed-goodies/tag-column-width 18)
  (elfeed-goodies/setup))

(after! elfeed-tube
  (setq elfeed-tube-auto-fetch-p t
        elfeed-tube-auto-save-p nil
        elfeed-tube-fields '(duration captions chapters description)
        elfeed-tube-backend 'yt-dlp))

(use-package! elfeed-tube-mpv
  :after elfeed-tube)
#+end_src

** PowerShell
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package! powershell
  :mode ("\\.ps1\\'" . powershell-mode)
  :config
  (defun my/powershell-run-file ()
    "Run current .ps1 file."
    (interactive)
    (unless buffer-file-name (user-error "Buffer has no file"))
    (save-buffer)
    (compile (format "pwsh -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File \"%s\""
                     (expand-file-name buffer-file-name))))

  (defun my/powershell-run-region ()
    "Send region or current line to inferior PowerShell."
    (interactive)
    (let* ((beg (if (use-region-p) (region-beginning) (line-beginning-position)))
           (end (if (use-region-p) (region-end)       (line-end-position)))
           (code (buffer-substring-no-properties beg end)))
      (unless (get-buffer "*PowerShell*") (powershell))
      (comint-send-string (get-buffer-process "*PowerShell*") (concat code "\n"))
      (display-buffer "*PowerShell*")))

  (map! :map powershell-mode-map
        "C-c C-c" #'my/powershell-run-file
        "C-c C-r" #'my/powershell-run-region
        "C-c C-z" #'powershell))
#+end_src

* Session persistence (desktop)
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
;; 会话持久化：记住上次的窗口布局 / 打开的文件 / 光标位置，下次启动自动恢复。
;; 配合 Windows 登录自启（HKCU Run → runemacs.exe）使用。
;; 想临时不恢复：runemacs.exe --no-desktop
(let ((dir (expand-file-name "desktop/" doom-cache-dir)))
  (unless (file-directory-p dir)
    (make-directory dir t))
  (setq desktop-dirname        dir
        desktop-path           (list dir)
        desktop-base-file-name ".emacs.desktop"
        desktop-base-lock-name ".emacs.desktop.lock"))

(setq desktop-save                       t   ; 退出时无条件保存，不弹问询
      desktop-load-locked-desktop        t   ; 上次异常退出留下的 lock 也直接加载
      desktop-auto-save-timeout          300 ; 空闲 5 分钟自动存一次（防强杀 / 断电）
      desktop-restore-frames             t   ; 恢复 frame 位置、大小、分屏
      desktop-restore-reuses-frames      t
      desktop-restore-forces-onscreen    t
      desktop-restore-in-current-display nil
      desktop-lazy-idle-delay            3   ; 后台分批把剩余 buffer 加载完
      desktop-lazy-verbose               nil)

;; 内部 / 临时 buffer 不写进 desktop（保留默认值，再追加）
(setq desktop-buffers-not-to-save
      (concat "\\` "
              "\\|\\*\\(straight-process\\|straight-byte-compile\\|doom\\|doom:\\|"
              "Messages\\|Async.*\\|ielm\\|e?shell.*\\|vterm.*\\|PowerShell\\|"
              "compilation\\|Org .*\\)\\*"
              "\\|\\.log\\'"))

;; 2026-10-22 停用：会话恢复交给 easysession（见下一节）。
;; 两者功能重叠且会各自按自己的顺序抢着摆窗口布局，不能同时开。
;; 上面这段配置完整保留，方便回滚 —— 想退回 desktop 只要改回 `(desktop-save-mode 1)'
;; 并把下一节的 easysession 块注释掉。旧的 ~/.emacs.d/.local/cache/desktop/
;; .emacs.desktop 也留着不动（desktop 停用后不再更新，留作回滚时的备份）。
(desktop-save-mode -1)
#+end_src

* Session & buffers (2026-10-22)
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
;;; Session & buffers
;; 来源：jamescherti《Essential Emacs Packages》清单评审。整套 90 多项，
;; 扣掉写代码向的（LSP / 格式化 / 折叠 / 各语言 mode）和 Doom 已自带的，
;; 剩下就是这三个。

;; persist-text-scale: 文字缩放跨会话保持。
;; Doom 的 text-zoom（SPC z x / SPC z z）是纯 buffer-local，重开 Emacs 就回到
;; 默认值；这个 minor mode 把每个文件的 text-scale-mode-amount 落盘持久化。
(use-package! persist-text-scale
  :config
  (persist-text-scale-mode 1))

;; buffer-terminator: 自动回收长时间闲置的 buffer。
;; 动机：roam 361 个 + daily 236 个文件，会话挂久了 SPC b b 会被几百个 buffer
;; 淹没；而且每个 buffer 都拖着自己的 buffer-local mode 和 timer，
;; Emacs 单线程，timer 数量上去了直接反映成卡顿。
;; 保守取值：45 分钟无交互才回收（默认 30），每 15 分钟扫一次（默认 10）。
;; kill 前会检查未保存修改、进程 buffer 等，默认规则足够安全；
;; 真要更细的保留/回收策略，改 buffer-terminator-rules-alist。
(use-package! buffer-terminator
  :config
  (setq buffer-terminator-verbose            nil
        buffer-terminator-inactivity-timeout (* 45 60)
        buffer-terminator-interval           (* 15 60))
  (buffer-terminator-mode 1))

(use-package! easysession
  :demand t
  :config
  ;; 会话文件放 ~/.emacs.d/.local/cache/easysession/，与 desktop/ 同级且同样被 gitignore
  (setq easysession-directory (expand-file-name "easysession/" doom-cache-dir)

        ;; ⚠ 默认是 nil —— 不设的话只在「退出 Emacs」时保存一次，
        ;;   崩溃 / 强杀 / systemctl kill 就全丢。这里对齐 desktop 的
        ;;   desktop-auto-save-timeout 300，改成 10 分钟一次。
        easysession-save-interval (* 10 60)

        ;; 启动时自动加载上次会话（等价于 desktop-read）
        easysession-setup-load-session t

        ;; 切换会话前先存当前会话
        easysession-switch-to-save-session t
        easysession-switch-to-exclude-current nil

        ;; 记录所有打开的 buffer（= desktop 的行为，也是包的默认值）。
        ;; 2026-10-23 更正：前一天我设成了 #'easysession-visible-buffer-list
        ;; （只记看得见的），理由是让它跟 buffer-terminator 同向 —— 结果是重启后
        ;; 140 个文件一个都没回来，只能靠 M-x desktop-read 手工救。
        ;; 实测你要的就是「全回来」，别再为了跟别的包讲和而降级这里。
        easysession-buffer-list-function #'buffer-list)

  ;; 入口是 setup，不是 save-mode：它负责挂 emacs-startup-hook（daemon 下是
  ;; server-after-make-frame-hook）做启动自动加载 + 定期保存 + 退出时保存。
  (easysession-setup))

;; 常用命令（暂时不加 leader 键位，先看一段时间哪些真的用得上）：
;;   M-x easysession-switch-to                       切换会话
;;   M-x easysession-switch-to-and-restore-geometry  切换并恢复窗口几何
;;   M-x easysession-save / -rename / -delete / -unload
;;   M-x easysession-reset                           清空 buffer / frame / window
#+end_src

* Email (mu4e + mbsync)
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
;;; Email (mu4e + mbsync)
;;
;; 外部依赖（系统级，必须用 root 装，Doom 装不了）：
;;   Arch/Omarchy:  sudo pacman -S isync  &&  yay -S mu
;;                  （mu 不在官方仓库，只有 AUR；isync 提供 mbsync）
;;   Debian/Ubuntu: sudo apt install isync maildir-utils
;;     isync          → mbsync，负责 IMAP 双向同步
;;     mu / maildir-utils → mu（索引/查询）；mu4e（Emacs 界面 elisp）由 Doom 装
;;
;; 数据流：  邮件服务器 --mbsync--> ~/.mail/{gmail,outlook} (Maildir)
;;                                  --mu index--> Xapian 索引 --mu4e--> Emacs
;;
;; 密码放在 ~/.authinfo.gpg（GPG 加密，直接用 emacs 打开编辑，保存自动重加密）。
;; mbsync 通过 ~/.local/bin/authinfo-get 解密取密码 —— 配置文件里没有明文。
;; 配置文件：
;;   ~/.mbsyncrc              IMAP 同步规则（Gmail + Outlook 两个 Channel）
;;   ~/.authinfo.gpg          凭据
;;   ~/.local/bin/authinfo-get 解密取密码的小脚本
;;
;; 首次使用流程（按顺序跑一次）：
;;   1. emacs ~/.authinfo.gpg   把 CHANGE_ME_GMAIL / CHANGE_ME_OUTLOOK 换成应用专用密码
;;   2. mkdir -p ~/.mail/gmail ~/.mail/outlook
;;   3. mbsync --all            首次全量同步，几万封邮件可能要几十分钟
;;   4. mu init --maildir ~/.mail \
;;          --my-address yuanxiang424@gmail.com \
;;          --my-address yuan_xiang@outlook.com
;;      mu index
;;   5. SPC o m 打开 mu4e（SPC o M 写新邮件）
;;
;; 日常：mu4e 每 5 分钟自动跑一次 mbsync --all + mu index。

;; Ubuntu 把 mu4e 的 elisp 装到 /usr/share/emacs/site-lisp/mu4e，
;; 而自编的 Emacs 31（prefix=/usr/local）不会自动把 /usr/share/... 放进 load-path，
;; 不补这一行 (require 'mu4e) 直接失败、整个模块静默哑火。
(dolist (dir (append (file-expand-wildcards "/usr/share/emacs/site-lisp/*mu4e*")
                     (file-expand-wildcards "/usr/local/share/emacs/site-lisp/*mu4e*")))
  (when (file-directory-p dir)
    (add-to-list 'load-path dir)))

(after! mu4e
  (setq mu4e-root-maildir "~/.mail")
  (when (boundp 'mu4e-maildir)
    (setq mu4e-maildir "~/.mail"))

  (setq
   ;; mbsync 移动邮件会改文件名，mu4e 必须跟着改，否则索引与文件对不上
   mu4e-change-filenames-when-moving t
   ;; 每 5 分钟自动 mbsync + reindex；嫌吵改 nil，然后手动 U 更新
   mu4e-update-interval 300
   mu4e-get-mail-command "mbsync --all"
   ;; Gmail 用标签而不是文件夹，邮件不会真的"移动"，可以做懒检查
   mu4e-index-cleanup nil
   mu4e-index-lazy-check t
   ;; 收件人显示为名字而不是一长串地址
   mu4e-view-show-addresses t
   mu4e-compose-dont-reply-to-self t
   mu4e-user-mail-address-list '("yuanxiang424@gmail.com"
                                 "yuan_xiang@outlook.com"))

  ;; +gmail flag 靠 maildir 根名含 "gmail" 自动识别；这里显式登记一遍更保险
  (setq +mu4e-gmail-accounts '(("yuanxiang424@gmail.com" . "/gmail")))

  (setq mu4e-contexts
        (list
         ;; ---------------- Gmail ----------------
         (make-mu4e-context
          :name "Gmail"
          :match-func (lambda (msg)
                        (when msg
                          (string-prefix-p "/gmail"
                                           (mu4e-message-field msg :maildir))))
          :vars '((user-mail-address     . "yuanxiang424@gmail.com")
                  (user-full-name        . "Sean Yuan")
                  (mu4e-sent-folder      . "/gmail/[Gmail]/Sent Mail")
                  (mu4e-drafts-folder    . "/gmail/[Gmail]/Drafts")
                  (mu4e-trash-folder     . "/gmail/[Gmail]/Trash")
                  ;; Gmail 没有真正的"归档"目录，/gmail/Archive 是一个自建标签。
                  ;; mbsync 把它 CREATE 到服务器上（Gmail 会变成一个 label），
                  ;; 移进去就等于去掉 INBOX 标签 = 归档。若 Gmail 拒绝建目录，
                  ;; 改成 "/gmail/[Gmail]/All Mail"（代价是索引里出现重复邮件）。
                  (mu4e-refile-folder    . "/gmail/Archive")
                  (smtpmail-smtp-server  . "smtp.gmail.com")
                  (smtpmail-smtp-service . 587)
                  (smtpmail-stream-type  . starttls)
                  (smtpmail-smtp-user    . "yuanxiang424@gmail.com")))
         ;; --------------- Outlook ---------------
         (make-mu4e-context
          :name "Outlook"
          :match-func (lambda (msg)
                        (when msg
                          (string-prefix-p "/outlook"
                                           (mu4e-message-field msg :maildir))))
          :vars '((user-mail-address     . "yuan_xiang@outlook.com")
                  (user-full-name        . "Sean Yuan")
                  ;; Outlook 个人账号的目录名取决于网页界面语言：
                  ;; 英文是 Sent/Drafts/Deleted，中文界面可能是「已发送邮件」等。
                  ;; 跑完 mbsync --all 后 ls ~/.mail/outlook 看实际名字再改。
                  (mu4e-sent-folder      . "/outlook/Sent")
                  (mu4e-drafts-folder    . "/outlook/Drafts")
                  (mu4e-trash-folder     . "/outlook/Deleted")
                  (mu4e-refile-folder    . "/outlook/Archive")
                  (smtpmail-smtp-server  . "smtp-mail.outlook.com")
                  (smtpmail-smtp-service . 587)
                  (smtpmail-stream-type  . starttls)
                  (smtpmail-smtp-user    . "yuan_xiang@outlook.com")))))

  (setq mu4e-bookmarks
        '((:name "未读"            :query "flag:unread AND NOT flag:trashed" :key ?u)
          (:name "今天"            :query "date:today..now"                  :key ?t)
          (:name "最近 7 天"        :query "date:7d..now"                     :key ?w)
          (:name "Gmail 收件箱"     :query "maildir:/gmail/INBOX"             :key ?g)
          (:name "Outlook 收件箱"   :query "maildir:/outlook/INBOX"           :key ?o)
          (:name "带附件"          :query "mime:application/* OR mime:image/*" :key ?a)
          (:name "Gmail 已发送"     :query "maildir:\"/gmail/[Gmail]/Sent Mail\"" :key ?G)
          (:name "Outlook 已发送"   :query "maildir:/outlook/Sent"            :key ?O))))

;; 有些邮件服务商（含 Outlook）发的 HTML 邮件肉眼没法看，优先看 text/plain
(with-eval-after-load 'mm-decode
  (setq mm-discouraged-alternatives '("text/html" "text/richtext")
        mm-text-html-renderer 'shr))
#+end_src

* Keybindings
** Leader
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(map! :leader
      ;; Gcal
      (:prefix ("G" . "gcal")
       :desc "Sync"   "s" #'org-gcal-sync
       :desc "Fetch"  "f" #'org-gcal-fetch
       :desc "Delete" "d" #'org-gcal-delete-at-point
       :desc "Push"   "p" #'my/org-gcal-auto-post)

      ;; Open
      (:prefix ("o" . "open")
       :desc "Elfeed"           "e" #'elfeed
       :desc "Eshell here"      "E" #'my/eshell-here
       :desc "Daily agenda"     "d" (cmd! (org-agenda nil "d"))
       :desc "Weekly agenda"    "w" (cmd! (org-agenda nil "w"))
       :desc "GTD review"       "g" (cmd! (org-agenda nil "g"))
       :desc "Beancount ledger" "$" #'my/open-beancount-main
       :desc "EPUB reader"      "R" #'my/epub-reader-open-dwim
       :desc "Mail (mu4e)"      "m" #'mu4e
       :desc "Compose mail"     "M" #'mu4e-compose-new)

      ;; Notes（denote 前缀 2026-10-17 删除，journal 两个键收进这里）
      (:prefix ("n" . "notes")
       :desc "Consult notes (pick)"   "s" #'consult-notes
       ;; 2026-10-22 换自家版本: 上游的会把 org-roam-directory 整个目录塞进 rg,
       ;; 236 个 daily 密文刷屏。自家版用文件列表, 覆盖一致但排除 daily。
       ;; (2026-10-24 晚: 日记已取消加密, 但仍排除 —— 用 my/journal-search
       ;;  专门搜日记更快, 见下面 J。)
       ;; (2026-11-06: 日记恢复加密, 更该排除 —— rg 扫密文会刷 "binary file matches"。)
       :desc "Consult notes (search)" "S" #'my/consult-notes-search-in-all-notes
       ;; 2026-10-22 换自家版本: 上游 `org-ql-find-in-org-directory' 不递归,
       ;; 实测只搜到 ~/org/inbox.org 一个文件。自家版走 `my/org-ql-files'。
       :desc "Org-QL find · 全库(排日记)" "q" #'my/org-ql-find-notes
       :desc "Today's journal"         "j" #'my/journal-today
       ;; 2026-10-22 加: Doom 的 dailies 键 `SPC m m d' 只在 org buffer 里能用,
       ;; 这里补一个全局入口 -> `my/journal-goto-date' (不存在提示, 不新建)。
       ;; 2026-10-24 改: 键位 d -> **g**。d 让给 denote 前缀, 这里改用
       ;; g = goto, 与描述里的「跳到」也对得上。
       ;; 2026-10-27: 笔记前缀换回 `SPC n r', `d' 重新空出 —— 但 g 保持不变。
       :desc "Goto journal date · 跳到某天" "g" #'my/journal-goto-date
       :desc "Insert weather · 补天气" "W" #'my/journal-insert-weather
       ;; C-u 前缀 = 只搜最近 90 天
       ;; (2026-10-24 晚: 原注释说「全量要解密 236 个文件, 实测 ~40s」——
       ;;  日记取消加密后是纯文本 I/O, 毫秒级, 全量搜也不卡了)
       ;; (2026-11-06: 恢复加密, 但只剩 1 个 86KB 文件, 解密一次 ~0.1s, 依旧不卡)
       :desc "Search journal · 搜日记"  "J" #'my/journal-search
       ;; 2026-10-27 三改: `SPC n N/E/L' 改绑自研 pass-through。
       ;; denote-journal 包已退役，但日记的三个动作要保留手感:
       ;;   N = new my/journal--new-file + 打开：幂等，已有就不建第二篇
       ;;      （旧的 denote-journal-new-entry 无条件新建, 一天可能多篇）
       ;;   E = 今天的日记（不存在就建）—— 日常最常用, 等价于 N
       ;;      （自研版本天然就是 new-or-existing, 两者合并）
       ;;   L = 删。这条原是「从别处链到今天的日记」, 用得非常少, 而一旦要 link
       ;;      还得处理 `(denote:' vs `id:' 链接形态 —— 现在走 `id:', 直接手打
       ;;      `[[id:...]]' 或用 `SPC n r i' 插 node link 就行, 不值得单占一个键。
       ;;      ⚠ 空出来的 L **不复用**——留着, 免得将来又想起旧语义时撞车。
       :desc "Journal today · 今天的日记"  "E" #'my/journal-today
       :desc "Journal new · 新建(幂等)"    "N" #'my/journal-today
       (:prefix ("i" . "investment · 投资")
        :desc "Beancount buy · 买入"                  "b" #'my/beancount-record-buy
        :desc "Beancount sell · 卖出"                 "s" #'my/beancount-record-sell
        :desc "Beancount prices · 批量记录价格"        "p" #'my/beancount-record-price-batch
        :desc "Beancount price · 单个记录价格"         "P" #'my/beancount-record-price
        :desc "Beancount balance · 持仓校验"          "B" #'my/beancount-record-balance
        :desc "Beancount balances · 批量持仓校验"     "V" #'my/beancount-record-balance-batch
        :desc "Update prices latest · 更新近期价格"   "u" #'my/beancount-update-prices-latest
        :desc "Rebuild price history · 重建历史价格"  "H" #'my/beancount-rebuild-prices-history
        :desc "Open ledger · 打开账本"                "o" #'my/open-beancount-main
        :desc "Check ledger · 检查账本"               "c" #'my/beancount-check-main
        :desc "Fava · 打开报表"                       "f" #'my/beancount-fava-main
        :desc "Bank wealth redeem · 银行理财赎回"     "r" #'my/beancount-record-bank-redeem
        :desc "Pension fund buy · 养老金基金买入"     "y" #'my/beancount-record-pension-buy
        :desc "CMB cashflow · 招行转入转出"          "C" #'my/beancount-record-cmb-cashflow
        :desc "Bank interest · 银行结息收入"         "i" #'my/beancount-record-bank-interest
        :desc "Collateral transfer · 担保品划转"      "t" #'my/beancount-record-collateral-transfer
        :desc "Margin buy · 融资买入"                "m" #'my/beancount-record-margin-buy
        :desc "Margin sell · 融资卖出"               "M" #'my/beancount-record-margin-sell)
       (:prefix ("n" . "capture · 速记")
        :desc "Task · 任务"        "t" (cmd! (org-capture nil "t"))
        :desc "Inbox · 收件箱"     "i" (cmd! (org-capture nil "i"))
        :desc "Journal · 日志"     "j" (cmd! (org-capture nil "j"))
        :desc "Append · 追加"      "a" (cmd! (org-capture nil "a"))
        (:prefix ("m" . "media · 影音")
         :desc "电影·想看"  "m" (cmd! (org-capture nil "mm"))
         :desc "电影·看完"  "M" (cmd! (org-capture nil "mM"))
         :desc "剧·想看"    "t" (cmd! (org-capture nil "mt"))
         :desc "剧·看完"    "T" (cmd! (org-capture nil "mT"))
         :desc "动漫·想看"  "a" (cmd! (org-capture nil "ma"))
         :desc "动漫·看完"  "A" (cmd! (org-capture nil "mA")))
        (:prefix ("b" . "books · 书")
         :desc "书·待读"    "b" (cmd! (org-capture nil "bb"))
         :desc "书·阅读中"  "B" (cmd! (org-capture nil "bB"))))
       ;; 2026-10-18: Doom 官方把 roam 键位绑在 org-mode-map 的 localleader（`SPC m m`），
       ;; 只在 org 文件里有效；在 dashboard / scratch / 任何非 org buffer 里会是
       ;; "SPC m m is undefined"。这里补一组**全局** leader 键位，任何地方都能唤起。
       ;;
       ;; ⚠ 2026-10-22 修正: 这段原先缩进在 `(:prefix ("n" . "capture · 速记"))` **内部**,
       ;;   实际路径是 `SPC n n r' 而不是设想的 `SPC n r' (实测后者 UNBOUND)。
       ;;   `map!' 的嵌套层级由括号结构决定 —— 带描述的 :prefix 只影响同级键的保留与否,
       ;;   不会改变层级; 错一级就整体下沉一层, 且不报任何错。改完必须实测 key-binding,
       ;;   不能只看 tangle 通过。
       ;;
       ;; ⚠ 2026-10-27: 前缀键 **换回 `r'**（Sean 拍板，推翻我「留在 d」的判断）。
       ;;   键位变迁史: roam 时代 `r' → 2026-10-24 denote 时代改 `d' → 2026-10-27
       ;;   roam 回归又换回 `r'。我原先的理由是「别跟着包来回搬，键位是人的接口」；
       ;;   现在包也回来了，`r' = roam 是自洽的 —— 用不着在脑子里多做一次
       ;;   「d 其实是 roam」的映射。代价是这次肌肉记忆第三次改，认了。
       ;;   ⚠ 空出来的 `d' **不复用** —— 留着, 免得将来再翻一次烧饼。
       ;;
       ;; 命令对照（2026-10-27 版）:
       ;;   f org-roam-node-find         找笔记 / 不存在则新建
       ;;   i org-roam-node-insert       插 [[id:]] 链接
       ;;   b org-roam-buffer-toggle     反链 buffer
       ;;   c my/roam-capture-to-domain  新建笔记（**会问领域目录**，原生 capture 落根目录）
       ;;   g org-roam-graph             图谱
       ;;   s org-roam-db-sync           重建 sqlite 索引
       ;;   x my/org-roam-extract-to-domain  heading 抽成独立笔记（同样先问目录）
       ;;   t/T my/roam-tag-add/-remove  加/删标签（写 `#+filetags:'，候选 = 白名单）
       ;;   w/W/S literature            网页存档三入口（与笔记系统无关，常驻）
       ;;
       ;; ⚠ `s' 的含义变了两次，别记混:
       ;;   roam 时代 = db-sync → denote 时代 = org-id-update-id-locations →
       ;;   现在回来 = db-sync。**两个都还是有用的**: db-sync 管 roam 的 node 索引，
       ;;   org-id-update-id-locations 管 `[[id:]]` 链接的真索引。后者在
       ;;   `SPC n r s' 换回 db-sync 后没有入口了 —— 需要时手动
       ;;   `M-x my/org-id-update-id-locations'（见 org-id 块里的 advice 版）。
       (:prefix ("r" . "roam · 笔记")
        :desc "Find node · 找笔记"   "f" #'org-roam-node-find
        ;; roam 原生 `org-roam-node-insert' 产 [[id:]] 链接 —— 正好是本库存量链接
        ;; 的形态（roam→denote 迁移时保留了 42 条 [[id:]]，没转 [[denote:]]）。
        ;; ⚠ 不再需要 denote 时代的 i / I 拆分: 那个区分来自 denote 的
        ;;   `link' vs `link-to-heading' 两个 **不同的链接协议**。roam 只产 id 链接，
        ;;   要不要带 heading 是在 minibuffer 里选的（同一个命令）。
        ;;   I 键**不解绑用新命令**，留空以避免误绑错 sexp。
        :desc "Insert link · 插链接"  "i" #'org-roam-node-insert
        :desc "Backlinks · 反链"     "b" #'org-roam-buffer-toggle
        ;; ⚠ 绑自研 `my/roam-capture-to-domain' 而不是原生 `org-roam-capture':
        ;;   原生 file 模板写 `%<%Y%m%dT%H%M%S>-${slug}.org' 且**落根目录**，两个问题 ——
        ;;   ① roam 原生 slug 吃掉全角标点, 与库存命名不一致；
        ;;   ② 落根 = 脱离 notes/<domain>/ 分域。
        :desc "Capture · 新建笔记"   "c" #'my/roam-capture-to-domain
        :desc "Sync db · 重建索引"   "s" #'org-roam-db-sync
        :desc "Graph · 图谱"         "g" #'org-roam-graph
        ;; 2026-10-22 加 / 2026-10-24 改 / 2026-10-27 再改: tag 入口换成自研
        ;; `my/roam-tag-add' / `my/roam-tag-remove'。不用的两个原生命令各有硬伤:
        ;;   `org-set-tags-command' —— 在 before-first-heading（本库 file-per-node
        ;;     的常态）直接 user-error「Setting file tags is not supported yet」
        ;;     (org.el:12241，源码挂着 FIXME)。
        ;;   `org-roam-tag-add'     —— 候选掺 db 里 24 个野生 tag，自毁白名单。
        ;; 自研版 = roam 的写入路径 + 白名单候选，详见 org-roam 块的 tag 段。
        :desc "Add tag · 加标签"    "t" #'my/roam-tag-add
        :desc "Remove tag · 删标签" "T" #'my/roam-tag-remove
        ;; 2026-10-21 加 / 2026-10-27 改回: 抽取 subtree 成独立笔记。
        ;; 用自研 `my/org-roam-extract-to-domain' 会先问领域目录 —— 原生
        ;; `org-roam-extract-subtree' 的 file-name 预填锁死在 org-roam-directory 根。
        :desc "Extract · heading 成笔记" "x" #'my/org-roam-extract-to-domain
        ;; 2026-10-23 加: 网页存档。URL 从剪贴板取 (elfeed/eww 里直接按 L)。
        :desc "Literature · 网页存档"   "w" #'my/literature-save
        ;; 2026-10-23: 外部浏览器里看到想存的两个入口。W = 剪贴板富文本 (不联网,
        ;; 反爬站点可用), S = Ctrl+S 另存的 HTML (最保真, 连图都在)。
        :desc "Literature · 剪贴板网页" "W" #'my/literature-save-clipboard
        :desc "Literature · 本地 HTML"  "S" #'my/literature-save-file))

      ;; Toggle / utilities
      (:prefix ("t" . "toggle")
       ;; :desc "Focus writing"         "F" #'my/focus-writing-toggle
       )

      :desc "Visual undo tree" "u" #'vundo
      :desc "org-noter"        "N" #'org-noter)
#+end_src

** Org localleader
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(map! :after org
      :map org-mode-map
      :localleader
      (:prefix ("i" . "image")
       :desc "Preview near point" "t" #'my/org-preview-images-near-point
       :desc "Attach menu"    "a" #'my/org-attach-menu
       ;; p / s 走 org-download, 但 org-download-method = 'attach, 所以
       ;; 一样落到 org-attach 目录 + [[attachment:]] 链接
       :desc "Paste clipboard" "p" #'org-download-clipboard
       :desc "Screenshot"     "s" #'org-download-screenshot
       :desc "Yank"           "y" #'org-download-yank
       :desc "Delete file"   "d" #'my/org-delete-file-link-at-point
       :desc "Rotate file"  "r" #'my/org-rotate-image-file
       :desc "Open external" "o" #'my/org-image-open-external
       :desc "Zoom in"       "+" #'my/org-image-resize-up
       :desc "Zoom out"      "-" #'my/org-image-resize-down
       :desc "Caption"       "c" #'my/org-image-caption
       :desc "Preview links" "l" #'org-link-preview
       :desc "Compress→webp" "O" #'my/org-image-optimize
       :desc "Grab http img" "D" #'my/org-image-download-link-at-point
       :desc "Insert figure" "f" #'my/org-insert-image-figure
       :desc "Dedupe imgs"   "x" #'my/org-dedupe-inline-images
       :desc "Number captions" "n" #'my/org-caption-number-mode)
      ;; NOTE: Doom 自带 (:prefix ("d" . "date/deadline"))，带描述的 :prefix 会
      ;; 清空该前缀原有键，所以 date 那几个键必须在这里显式补回来。
      ;; 2026-10-17: denote 那 6 个键 + dblock 子组随包一起删除, 前缀改名 date/id。
      ;; `I' 是大文件体系下加 heading CUSTOM_ID 的命令, 跟 denote 无关, 保留。
      (:prefix ("d" . "date/id")
       ;; org date/deadline（原 Doom 默认，勿删）
       :desc "Schedule"               "s" #'org-schedule
       :desc "Deadline"               "d" #'org-deadline
       :desc "Timestamp"              "t" #'org-time-stamp
       :desc "Inactive timestamp"     "T" #'org-time-stamp-inactive
       ;; 大文件体系下的 heading 级 ID (C-u 前缀 = 同时加进顶部导航)
       :desc "Set heading CUSTOM_ID"  "I" #'my/org-heading-id)
      ;; org-transclusion
      (:prefix ("T" . "transclusion")
       :desc "Add at point"    "a" #'org-transclusion-add
       :desc "Add all"         "A" #'org-transclusion-add-all
       :desc "Remove at point" "r" #'org-transclusion-remove
       :desc "Toggle mode"     "t" #'org-transclusion-mode)
      :desc "TODO state"         "t" #'org-todo
      :desc "Paste rich text"    "V" #'my/org-paste-rich
      :desc "Focus writing"      "F" #'my/focus-writing-toggle
      :desc "Rebucket entry"     "R" #'my/org-rebucket-current-entry
      :desc "Archive done tasks" "A" #'my/org-archive-done-tasks)
#+end_src

* Late fixes
** C-c C-c in =*Org Note*= popup
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(defadvice! my/org-ctrl-c-ctrl-c-note-fix (fn &rest args)
  :around #'org-ctrl-c-ctrl-c
  (if (string= (buffer-name) "*Org Note*")
      (org-store-log-note)
    (apply fn args)))
#+end_src

** Smart Input Source (sis) + Rime — Windows
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(when (featurep :system 'windows)
  (use-package! sis
    :demand t
    :config
    ;; Skip sis's auto-detect; wire Windows IME API directly.
    (setq sis-english-source nil
          sis-other-source   t
          sis-do-get         #'w32-get-ime-open-status
          sis-do-set         #'w32-set-ime-open-status)

    ;; Disable 0.2s idle poll (races imm32.dll on buffer creation).
    (setq sis-auto-refresh-seconds nil)

    (defun sis-switch ()
      "Switch between English and other, driven by the Windows IME API."
      (interactive)
      (setq sis--for-buffer-locked nil)
      (if (w32-get-ime-open-status)
          (sis--set-english)
        (sis--set-other)))

    (sis-global-cursor-color-mode t)
    (when (fboundp 'modus-themes-get-color-value)
      (setq sis-other-cursor-color   (modus-themes-get-color-value 'fg-alt)
            sis-default-cursor-color (modus-themes-get-color-value 'border)))

    (sis-global-respect-mode t)
    (sis-global-context-mode t)
    (sis-global-inline-mode  t)

    (setq sis-respect-go-english-triggers (list #'org-agenda))

    ;; Resync after frame regains focus
    (add-function :after after-focus-change-function
                  (lambda () (sis--get)))

    :hook
    (org-capture-mode . sis-set-other))

  ;; Bind the C-backslash key everywhere evil might grab keys first.
  ;; (注释里**不能**写裸反斜杠: Emacs reader 会把反斜杠+分号当转义序列,
  ;;  于是这个注释不结束, 后面的代码全被吞进注释, 最后报 "end-of-file"。
  ;;  org-babel tangle 不改转义层数, 但这个坑在 .el 里一样致命。)
  (define-key global-map (kbd "C-\\") #'sis-switch)
  (with-eval-after-load 'evil
    (define-key evil-normal-state-map (kbd "C-\\") #'sis-switch)
    (define-key evil-insert-state-map (kbd "C-\\") #'sis-switch)
    (define-key evil-motion-state-map (kbd "C-\\") #'sis-switch)
    (define-key evil-visual-state-map (kbd "C-\\") #'sis-switch)
    (define-key evil-emacs-state-map  (kbd "C-\\") #'sis-switch)))
#+end_src

** Smart Input Source (sis) + Rime — Linux (fcitx5)
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(when (and (featurep :system 'linux)
           (executable-find "fcitx5-remote"))
  (use-package! sis
    :demand t
    :config
    ;; fcitx5-remote prints "1" (inactive → keyboard-us) or "2" (active → rime).
    ;; `sis-ism-lazyman-config' points sis--ism at the fcitx5-remote binary and
    ;; wires do-set to -c / -o; do-get is derived from the same binary.
    (sis-ism-lazyman-config "1" "2" 'fcitx5)

    ;; 不轮询。sis 的 refresh 读的是 fcitx5 的**全局**状态 —— fcitx5-remote
    ;; 无参数时查的是当前焦点 IC, 而 Emacs 失焦时那是**别的窗口**的状态。
    ;; sis 拿到这个状态会按它 set 回去, 于是"不打字也会自己切英文":
    ;; 每秒一次读 + 写, 外部窗口的英文状态被反复灌进 fcitx5。
    ;; (实测: 30 秒里 21 次 State + 4 次 Deactivate, 全部来自这个 idle timer。)
    ;; 与 Windows 分支保持一致 (那里早就设 nil, 见上方)。
    ;; 代价: Emacs 不再自动感知外部切换 (如 Super+Space), 需手动切一次。
    (setq sis-auto-refresh-seconds nil)

    (sis-global-cursor-color-mode t)
    (when (fboundp 'modus-themes-get-color-value)
      (setq sis-other-cursor-color   (modus-themes-get-color-value 'fg-alt)
            sis-default-cursor-color (modus-themes-get-color-value 'border)))

    (sis-global-respect-mode t)
    (sis-global-context-mode t)
    (sis-global-inline-mode  t)

    (setq sis-respect-go-english-triggers (list #'org-agenda))

    ;; Resync after frame regains focus
    (add-function :after after-focus-change-function
                  (lambda () (sis--get)))

    :hook
    (org-capture-mode . sis-set-other))

  ;; NOTE: deliberately duplicating the Windows bindings above rather than
  ;; hoisting them into a shared helper — keeps either platform's block from
  ;; breaking the other. Fold them together once both sides are settled.
  (define-key global-map (kbd "C-\\") #'sis-switch)
  (with-eval-after-load 'evil
    (define-key evil-normal-state-map (kbd "C-\\") #'sis-switch)
    (define-key evil-insert-state-map (kbd "C-\\") #'sis-switch)
    (define-key evil-motion-state-map (kbd "C-\\") #'sis-switch)
    (define-key evil-visual-state-map (kbd "C-\\") #'sis-switch)
    (define-key evil-emacs-state-map  (kbd "C-\\") #'sis-switch)))
#+end_src
